### [1] [Efficient Training on Multiple Consumer GPUs with RoundPipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27085)
*Yibin Luo,Shiwei Gao,Huichuan Zheng,Youyou Lu,Jiwu Shu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出RoundPipe，一种面向消费级GPU的流水线并行调度方法，通过打破权重绑定约束实现近零气泡流水线，在8×RTX 4090上微调1.7B-32B模型加速1.48-2.16倍，并支持单服务器微调Qwen3-235B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级GPU上微调大语言模型（LLM）面临显存受限和PCIe互连速度慢的瓶颈。现有流水线并行（PP）结合CPU卸载方案存在权重绑定问题：将不均匀的模型阶段绑定到GPU会将流水线吞吐量限制为负载最重GPU的水平，导致严重的流水线气泡。

Method: RoundPipe将GPU视为无状态执行工作者池，采用轮询方式动态调度计算阶段，打破权重绑定约束。系统集成了优先级感知传输调度引擎、细粒度分布式事件同步协议以及自动化层划分算法，以确保训练正确性和系统效率。

Result: 在8×RTX 4090服务器上的评估表明，RoundPipe在微调1.7B至32B模型时，相比最先进基线实现1.48-2.16倍加速。值得注意，RoundPipe实现了在单服务器上以31K序列长度对Qwen3-235B模型进行LoRA微调。

Conclusion: RoundPipe有效解决了消费级GPU服务器上的权重绑定问题，实现了近零气泡流水线并行，显著提升了LLM微调效率，并已作为开源Python库公开发布。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) on consumer-grade GPUs is highly cost-effective, yet constrained by limited GPU memory and slow PCIe interconnects. Pipeline parallelism combined with CPU offloading mitigates these hardware bottlenecks by reducing communication overhead. However, existing PP schedules suffer from an inherent limitation termed the weight binding issue. Binding uneven model stages (e.g., the LM head is large) to GPUs limits the pipeline's throughput to that of the GPU with the heaviest load, leading to severe pipeline bubbles.
  In this paper, we propose RoundPipe, a novel pipeline schedule that breaks the weight binding constraint on consumer GPU servers. RoundPipe treats GPUs as a pool of stateless execution workers and dynamically dispatches computation stages across devices in a round-robin manner, achieving a near-zero-bubble pipeline. To ensure training correctness and system efficiency, RoundPipe integrates a priority-aware transfer scheduling engine, a fine-grained distributed event-based synchronization protocol, and an automated layer partitioning algorithm. Evaluations on an 8$\times$ RTX 4090 server demonstrate that RoundPipe achieves 1.48--2.16$\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art baselines when fine-tuning 1.7B to 32B models. Remarkably, RoundPipe enables LoRA fine-tuning of the Qwen3-235B model with 31K sequence length on a single server.
  RoundPipe is publicly available as an open-source Python library with comprehensive documentation.

</details>


### [2] [End-to-End and Phase-Level Performance Optimization for Hyperledger Fabric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27174)
*Pavan Sollu,Aniruddha Mukherjee,Divya Pulivarthi,S. R. Eshwar,Gugan Thoppe,Kshitij Pratihast,Tittu Varghese,Hrishikesh Nashikkar,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对Hyperledger Fabric企业级区块链，提出了两种提交阶段优化技术（块级流水线和策略性等待），并通过实验和仿真揭示了配置参数对性能的影响及其相互作用，为HLF提供了系统性的优化指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyperledger Fabric作为企业级许可链被广泛采用，但其吞吐量和延迟优化仍具挑战性，主要困难在于交易生命周期中某一阶段的优化决策可能对其他阶段产生负面影响，缺乏系统性的端到端研究。

Method: 采用生产级测试平台实验与校准的SimPy仿真相结合的方法，进行系统性的阶段级和端到端研究。提出两种提交阶段优化技术：块级流水线（重叠验证、私有数据获取与状态一致性检查）和策略性等待（协调提交进度）；同时对私有数据传播、区块大小选择和背书节点选择三个配置杠杆进行微基准测试和相互作用分析。

Result: 块级流水线将提交吞吐量提升高达1.9倍；策略性等待提升吞吐量1.2倍。实验发现：（1）私有数据传播的宽松仲裁显著降低背书和提交阶段延迟；（2）轻负载下小区块延迟更低，重负载下大区块提升吞吐量并降低延迟；（3）宽松的领导者选择显著减少丢弃交易并提升背书吞吐量。流水线提交的吞吐量增益在中等并行化水平时达到最大化。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了阶段感知和协议级别的HLF优化方案，揭示了配置参数间的复杂相互作用，为企业级区块链系统性能优化提供了系统性的理论指导和实践建议。

Abstract: Hyperledger Fabric (HLF) is a modular, permissioned blockchain widely adopted in enterprise settings. Enhancing its throughput and latency remains challenging, as optimization decisions made in one phase of the transaction lifecycle can adversely affect other phases. In this work, we present a systematic, phase-level and end-to-end study of HLF optimizations along three fronts, combining production-grade testbed experiments with calibrated SimPy simulations. First, we introduce two novel optimization techniques that target commit-phase bottlenecks: block-level pipelining and strategic waiting. In pipelining, we overlap validation and private-data acquisition of successive blocks with state-consistency checks and ledger updates improving commit throughput by up to 1.9x. Strategic waiting coordinates commit progress by temporarily pausing fast leaders and boosting laggers to sustain endorsement parallelism, yielding up to a 1.2x higher throughput. Second, we conduct micro-benchmarking of three configuration levers: private-data dissemination, block-size selection, and endorsement peer selection. Our results reveal that: (i) Relaxed quorums for private-data dissemination significantly reduce latency in both endorsement and commit phases; (ii) Under light workloads, smaller blocks yield lower end-to-end latency, whereas, under heavy workloads, larger blocks are necessary to improve throughput and reduce latency; and (iii) Relaxed leader selection dramatically reduces dropped transactions and boosts endorsement throughput, with a modest increase in MVCC invalidations. Finally, we analyze the interplay among private-data dissemination, VSCC parallelization, and pipelined commits. Interestingly, the throughput gains over a serial commit path are maximized at a moderate level of parallelization. Together, our findings provide phase-aware and protocol-level refinements for optimizing HLF.

</details>


### [3] [Towards the Democratization and Standardization of Dynamic Resources with MPI Spawning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27430)
*Sergio Iserte,Iker Martín-Alvarez,Krzystof Rojek,José I. Aliaga,Maribel Castillo,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个高效的动态资源管理工具，通过统一API和集成Proteo重配置引擎，实现了HPC环境下的灵活、自适应且用户友好的资源管理，解决了传统方法需要重启进程或缺乏RMS支持的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高性能计算（HPC）生产环境中，动态资源管理对于提升资源利用率和适应性至关重要。然而，现有方案存在灵活性不足、需要重启所有进程、缺乏资源管理系统（RMS）支持等局限性，限制了动态资源调整策略的多样性和实用性。

Method: 提出统一的动态资源管理API，使用户无需直接与底层DMR框架交互即可实现无缝集成；将DMR框架模块化，支持多种动态资源管理器；引入Proteo重配置引擎以提供丰富的重配置策略；开发可延展的MPDATA求解器版本以验证方案。

Result: 成功构建了支持多种重配置管理器的增强型模块化DMR框架；DMRlib通过集成Proteo引擎提供了广泛的重配置策略；MPDATA应用案例展示了该解决方案在性能表现和编码效率方面的优势，证明了无需重启所有进程即可实现多样化的重配置方法。

Conclusion: 该工具有效解决了传统动态资源管理的局限性，实现了灵活、自适应且用户友好的资源管理，为HPC应用提供了高效的动态资源调整能力，具有良好的实用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: This paper presents an efficient tool for managing dynamic resources in production high-performance computing (HPC) settings, focusing on flexibility, adaptability, and user-friendliness. We introduce a unified dynamic resource management application programming interface (API) that supports a wide range of HPC applications, allowing seamless integration without direct interaction with Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR). The DMR framework, evolved from the DMRlib structure, now supports various dynamic resource managers and includes the Proteo reconfiguration engine to enhance malleability strategies. This integration addresses previous limitations by allowing diverse reconfiguration methods without respawning all processes or lacking RMS support. The paper also showcases the solution's performance and coding productivity with the MPDATA (Multidimensional Positive Definite Advection Transport Algorithm) application. Key contributions include an enhanced modular DMR framework supporting different reconfiguration managers, upgraded DMRlib with the Proteo reconfiguration engine, offering extensive reconfiguration strategies, and a malleable version of the MPDATA solver.

</details>


### [4] [A Study on the Performance of Distributed Training of Data-driven CFD Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27431)
*Sergio Iserte,Alejandro González-Barberá,Paloma Barreda,Krzysztof Rojek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对流体模拟的时间序列预测深度学习模型，对比评估了仅CPU、多GPU和分布式三种训练方案的性能，证明了分布式GPU训练在保证高精度预测的同时能大幅减少训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于偏微分方程（PDE）求解的物理仿真方法计算开销巨大且耗时。虽然数据驱动方法利用AI技术可加速预测，但其训练阶段的成本不可忽略。因此需要评估不同的训练替代方案以优化训练效率。

Method: 以特定流体仿真为用例，构建时间序列预测深度学习模型来推理未来状态。通过少量代码适配，系统性地比较了仅CPU、多GPU及分布式三种不同训练实现方案的性能表现。

Result: 结果表明，分布式GPU训练方案相比其他实现具有显著优势，能够以计算流体动力学（CFD）求解器所需时间的很小一部分，实现高精度状态的预测。

Conclusion: 分布式GPU训练是数据驱动科学仿真中提升训练效率的有效途径，能在不影响预测精度的前提下大幅降低时间成本。

Abstract: Data-driven methods for computer simulations are blooming in many scientific areas. The traditional approach to simulating physical behaviors relies on solving partial differential equations (PDE). Since calculating these iterative equations is highly both computationally demanding and time-consuming, data-driven methods leverage artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to alleviate that workload. Data-driven methods have to be trained in advance to provide their subsequent fast predictions, however, the cost of the training stage is non-negligible. This paper presents a predictive model for inferencing future states of a specific fluid simulation that serves as a use case for evaluating different training alternatives. Particularly, this study compares the performance of only CPU, multiGPU, and distributed approaches for training a time series forecasting deep learning (DL) model. With some slight code adaptations, results show and compare, in different implementations, the benefits of distributed GPU-enabled training for predicting high-accuracy states in a fraction of the time needed by the computational fluid dynamics (CFD) solver.

</details>


### [5] [Back to the Future: Rethinking Endorsement in Order-Execute Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27659)
*Rongji Huang,Yifeng Ye,Gerui Wang,Mingchao Wan,Yuxing Duan,Jingjing Zhang,Guangtao Xue,Shengyun Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexTender框架，在保持传统order-execute架构不变的前提下，将灵活背书机制嵌入Tendermint共识协议中，解决了Hyperledger Fabric的EOV范式在高冲突负载下交易中止率高的问题，实现了最高10.6倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许可链系统（如ChainMaker）采用传统的order-execute架构，无法支持灵活背书；而Hyperledger Fabric的EOV范式虽支持灵活背书，但在DeFi等高冲突工作负载下存在交易中止率高的问题。研究动机是在不重构系统的前提下，将灵活背书集成到传统架构中，以更好地支持DeFi应用。

Method: 提出了一种新的框架，将灵活背书机制集成到经典的order-execute架构中。核心方法是在Tendermint BFT共识协议基础上实现FlexTender，通过将背书验证优雅地嵌入共识过程，在有序列表中确定性地移除有问题的交易，同时保持抗审查性和去中心化特性，且在正常情况下不产生额外的消息开销。

Result: 使用以太坊USDT工作负载进行实证评估，FlexTender相比同一平台上的EOV模拟实现了最高10.6倍的吞吐量提升，证明了该方法在高冲突场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: FlexTender成功地在传统order-execute架构中实现了灵活背书支持，避免了EOV范式的问题，同时保持了系统的去中心化和抗审查性。该方法为许可链系统提供了一种无需重构即可支持DeFi应用的有效解决方案，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Due to regulatory compliance and governance management, modern (permissioned) blockchains require flexible endorsement, which allows the endorsement policy for each contract or state object to be individually defined. To enable flexible endorsement, Hyperledger Fabric employs an execute-order-validate (EOV) paradigm, in which transactions first undergo speculative execution and endorsement, and are only then ordered and validated. Meanwhile, most blockchain systems, including the platform targeted in this work (i.e., ChainMaker), still follow a conflict-free order-execute framework. We argue that the EOV paradigm still faces several limitations, notably high abort rates in high-contention workloads such as those in Decentralized Finance (DeFi).
  To avoid refactoring our system and better suit DeFi applications, we try to integrate flexible endorsement into the classical order-execute architecture and accordingly propose a new framework. The key challenge is to deterministically remove problematic transactions from an ordered list, while preserving censorship resistance and decentralization for the remaining ones. We instantiate this framework on top of Tendermint, a seminal Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) protocol adopted in our system, and thereby propose FlexTender. By elegantly embedding endorsements into consensus, FlexTender incurs no additional messaging overhead in the normal case. Empirical evaluation using an Ethereum USDT workload demonstrates that FlexTender achieves up to $10.6\times$ speedup in throughput over an EOV simulation on the same platform.

</details>


### [6] [Monadic Presburger Predicates have Robust Population Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27767)
*Philipp Czerner,Javier Esparza,Vincent Fischer,Roland Guttenberg,Julian Pins,Simon Reilich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了一元Presburger算术可表达谓词存在鲁棒群体协议，并发现鲁棒性的状态复杂度代价至少为双指数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Lossin等人提出的关于群体协议对抗崩溃故障鲁棒性的开放问题，即是否所有Presburger谓词都存在鲁棒协议，本文旨在推进该问题的解决。

Method: 1. 证明所有可在一元Presburger算术中表达的谓词都存在鲁棒协议；2. 分析鲁棒性的状态复杂度代价，即比较给定谓词的最小鲁棒协议状态数与最小协议状态数的比率。

Result: 证明了所有可在一元Presburger算术中表达的谓词都存在鲁棒协议；发现鲁棒性的状态复杂度代价至少是谓词大小的双指数级；证明了Lossin等人针对阈值谓词x >= k的鲁棒协议具有最优状态复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作部分解决了Lossin等人提出的开放问题，展示了一元Presburger算术的可判定性，同时揭示了鲁棒性在状态复杂度上的显著代价。

Abstract: Population protocols are a model of distributed computation in which a collection of indistinguishable finite-state agents interact randomly in pairs to decide a predicate of their initial configuration. The agents decide by achieving a stable consensus on whether the predicate holds or not. It is known that population protocols can decide exactly the predicates expressible in Presburger arithmetic.
  Recently, Lossin et al. have introduced a notion of protocol robustness against adversarial crash failures. They show that all atomic Presburger predicates can be decided by robust protocols, and ask whether the same holds for every Presburger predicate. We make progress towards settling this question by proving that all predicates expressible in monadic Presburger arithmetic have robust protocols. In addition, we analyze the cost of robustness in terms of state complexity. We study the ratio between the number of states of the smallest robust protocol for a given predicate and the smallest protocol for it. We show that the cost of robustness is at least double exponential in the size of the predicate, and prove that the robust protocols by Lossin et al. for threshold predicates x >= k have optimal state complexity.

</details>


### [7] [ZipCCL: Efficient Lossless Data Compression of Communication Collectives for Accelerating LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27844)
*Wenxiang Lin,Xinglin Pan,Ruibo Fan,Shaohuai Shi,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ZipCCL，一个面向大语言模型训练的无损压缩通信库。通过利用训练数据近似高斯分布的特性，结合指数编码、GPU优化内核和自适应通信策略，在64 GPU集群上实现了最高1.35倍通信时间减少和1.18倍端到端训练加速，且不影响模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）分布式训练中，通信已成为关键瓶颈。尽管已有多种减少通信开销的方法，但无损压缩的潜力仍未被充分探索，因为传统的压缩和解压缩开销往往超过了减少通信流量带来的收益。作者观察到训练过程中的通信数据（包括激活值、梯度和参数）通常遵循近似高斯分布，这是数据压缩的关键特征。

Method: 提出了ZipCCL，一个面向LLM训练的无损压缩通信库，包含三项创新技术：(1) 理论基础的指数编码，利用LLM张量的高斯分布特性加速压缩，无需昂贵的在线统计计算；(2) GPU优化的压缩和解压缩内核，通过精心设计的内存访问模式和基于通信感知数据布局的流水线技术提升性能；(3) 自适应通信策略，根据工作负载模式和系统特性动态切换集合通信操作。

Result: 在64 GPU集群上使用混合专家模型（MoE）和密集Transformer模型进行评估，ZipCCL将通信时间减少了最高1.35倍，实现了最高1.18倍的端到端训练加速，且对模型质量没有任何影响。

Conclusion: ZipCCL证明了在LLM训练中利用数据分布特性进行高效无损压缩的可行性，为解决分布式训练中的通信瓶颈提供了新的技术路径，在保证模型质量的同时显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Communication has emerged as a critical bottleneck in the distributed training of large language models (LLMs). While numerous approaches have been proposed to reduce communication overhead, the potential of lossless compression has remained largely underexplored since compression and decompression typically consume larger overheads than the benefits of reduced communication traffic. We observe that the communication data, including activations, gradients and parameters, during training often follows a near-Gaussian distribution, which is a key feature for data compression. Thus, we introduce ZipCCL, a lossless compressed communication library of collectives for LLM training. ZipCCL is equipped with our novel techniques: (1) theoretically grounded exponent coding that exploits the Gaussian distribution of LLM tensors to accelerate compression without expensive online statistics, (2) GPU-optimized compression and decompression kernels that carefully design memory access patterns and pipeline using communication-aware data layout, and (3) adaptive communication strategies that dynamically switch collective operations based on workload patterns and system characteristics. Evaluated on a 64-GPU cluster using both mixture-of-experts and dense transformer models, ZipCCL reduces communication time by up to 1.35$\times$ and achieves end-to-end training speedups of up to 1.18$\times$ without any impact on model quality.

</details>


### [8] [AI Inference as Relocatable Electricity Demand: A Latency-Constrained Energy-Geography Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27855)
*Xubin Luo,Yang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种能源-地理框架，用于地理分布式AI推理的优化放置。通过放宽延迟约束，可以在不同地理位置间迁移计算任务，从而降低电力成本和碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI推理正成为一种持续且地理分布的电力需求源。与传统的电力负载不同，推理工作负载有时可以在远离用户服务位置的地方执行（在延迟、状态局部性、容量和监管约束允许的情况下）。本文旨在研究这种数字化的计算迁移何时可以被解释为延迟约束下的电力需求迁移。

Method: 开发了一个能源-地理框架，建模客户端、服务节点和计算节点的三层架构，将推理放置问题形式化为一个约束优化问题。该模型考虑了电力价格、边际碳强度、电力使用效率、计算容量、网络延迟和迁移摩擦等因素。核心概念是能源-延迟前沿，即通过放宽推理延迟预算所能解锁的边际成本和碳收益。

Result: 通过对代表性全球计算区域的透明风格化仿真，发现延迟放宽扩大了可行地理范围，而迁移摩擦、出口成本、状态局部性、法律约束和容量限制则会显著减少实际收益。不同延迟容忍度将工作负载分层为本地执行、区域执行和能源导向执行三层。

Conclusion: 该研究为地理分布式AI推理提供了一种新的优化方法，揭示了延迟约束与能源优化之间的权衡关系，为在满足延迟要求的同时实现电力成本和碳排放的降低提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: AI inference is becoming a persistent and geographically distributed source of electricity demand. Unlike many traditional electrical loads, inference workloads can sometimes be executed away from the user-facing service location, provided that latency, state locality, capacity, and regulatory constraints remain acceptable. This paper studies when such digital relocation of computation can be interpreted as latency-constrained relocation of electricity demand.
  We develop an energy-geography framework for geo-distributed AI inference. The framework models a three-layer architecture of clients, service nodes, and compute nodes, and formulates inference placement as a constrained optimization problem over electricity prices, marginal carbon intensity, power usage effectiveness, compute capacity, network latency, and migration frictions. The key object is the energy-latency frontier: the marginal cost and carbon benefit unlocked by relaxing inference latency budgets.
  The paper makes four contributions. First, it distinguishes physical electricity transmission from digital relocation of electricity-consuming computation. Second, it formulates a geo-distributed inference placement model with feasibility masks and migration frictions. Third, it introduces operational metrics, including relocatable inference demand, energy return on latency, carbon return on latency, and a relocation break-even condition. Fourth, it provides a transparent stylized simulation over representative global compute regions to show how heterogeneous latency tolerance separates workloads into local, regional, and energy-oriented execution layers.
  The results show that latency relaxation expands feasible geography, while migration frictions, egress costs, state locality, legal constraints, and capacity limits can sharply reduce realized benefits.

</details>


### [9] [AnTi-MiCS: Analytical Framework for Bounding Time in Embedded Mixed-Criticality Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27862)
*Behnaz Ranjbar,Akash Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对混合关键性系统中低关键性模式WCET确定的难题，提出了两种分析方法：单WCET方案AnTi-MiCS和多WCET方案MulTi-MiCS，在真实平台上验证平均提升QoS 30.27%，减少利用率浪费35.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合关键性系统中，高WCET虽为保守上界，但获取合适的低WCET以表征真实执行情况对提升处理器利用率和QoS至关重要。然而确定低关键性模式的低WCET存在挑战：过低的WCET虽可调度更多任务提升利用率，但会导致频繁模式切换降低QoS；过高的WCET虽减少模式切换保障QoS，却牺牲处理器利用率。这一权衡问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出两种设计时分析方法：(1) AnTi-MiCS：通过任务执行的设计时分析确定合适的单一低WCET；(2) MulTi-MiCS：针对执行时间分布变化大的场景（如双峰分布），利用后续输入之间的时间相关性，计算多个合适的低WCET，提供可扩展的解决方案。

Result: 在真实嵌入式实时基准平台上进行实验验证：与现有方法相比，所提方案平均提升QoS 30.27%，减少利用率浪费35.89%；MulTi-MiCS相比AnTi-MiCS进一步提升QoS 6.41%，减少利用率浪费8.23%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分析方法有效解决了混合关键性系统中低WCET确定的难题，在利用率和QoS之间实现了良好平衡，多WCET方案在处理复杂执行时间分布时表现更优，为MC系统设计提供了有效的WCET确定策略。

Abstract: In Mixed-Criticality (MC) systems, although the high Worst-Case Execution Time (WCET) serves as a conservative upper bound representing the task's maximum execution time under all conditions, obtaining a low WCET is essential for representing realistic executions and improving utilization and Quality-of-Service (QoS). Nevertheless, determining appropriate low WCET(s) for lower-criticality (LO) modes poses a significant challenge. Opting for a very low value of this WCET enhances processor utilization by scheduling more tasks in LO mode. Conversely, employing a larger WCET ensures fewer mode switches, thereby enhancing QoS, albeit at the cost of processor utilization. This paper proposes an analytical approach, AnTi-MiCS, to determine the appropriate low WCET through design-time analysis of task executions. In some cases, a single low WCET may not be adequate to capture large variations in the execution time distribution, for example, in scenarios like bimodal distributions. Therefore, we further propose a scalable approach, MulTi-MiCS, to compute multiple appropriate low WCETs. This approach exploits the temporal correlation between subsequent inputs presented to the application. Experimental results, conducted on a real platform with embedded real-time benchmarks, demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed scheme, in which QoS is improved by 30.27% on average while reducing utilization waste by 35.89%, compared to existing approaches. Besides, MulTi-MiCS improves QoS by 6.41% compared to AnTi-MiCS while reducing utilization waste by 8.23%.

</details>


### [10] [The Origins of MEV: Systematic Attribution of Arbitrage Opportunity Creation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27979)
*Andrei Seoev,Dmitry Belousov,Anastasiia Smirnova,Ksenia Kurinova,Aleksei Smirnov,Denis Fedyanin,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了MEV机会归因问题，提出了四种原子套利归因方法，并通过大规模回溯分析验证了MEV市场的高集中度和竞争性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注MEV的提取与缓解，但缺乏系统性的方法来追溯MEV机会的链上来源，无法有效识别哪些交易创造了套利机会及其贡献程度。

Method: 形式化MEV机会归因问题，设计并评估四种针对EVM兼容链上原子套利的归因方法：机器人数据驱动、基于仿真、基于系数和基于Shapley值的方法，并在Polygon链上超过一百万个区块中进行大规模回溯分析。

Result: 证明了大部分原子套利机会可追溯到单一来源交易，验证了竞争性MEV市场的核心假设；量化了MEV创造的高度集中分布，少数协议生成了大部分机会；提供了不同归因方法在准确性、成本和可扩展性方面的权衡分析。

Conclusion: 研究结果为协议设计者减少MEV泄漏、验证者优化交易排序以及分析师通过机会创造衡量生态系统健康提供了重要见解，有助于理解和治理MEV生态。

Abstract: Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) represents billions of dollars in extracted value that fundamentally shapes blockchain network dynamics and participant incentives. While research has focused on MEV extraction and mitigation, we lack systematic methods to attribute MEV opportunities to their on-chain origins. This paper formalizes the MEV opportunity attribution problem and introduces a systems framework for identifying which transactions create arbitrage opportunities and quantifying their contributions. We design and evaluate four attribution methods for atomic arbitrage on EVM-compatible networks: bot-data-driven, simulation-based, coefficient-based, and Shapley-based approaches. Through large-scale retrospective analysis spanning over one million blocks on Polygon, we demonstrate that the majority of atomic arbitrage opportunities can be traced to single source transactions, validating our central hypothesis about competitive MEV markets. We quantify a highly concentrated distribution of MEV creation, where a small subset of protocols generates most opportunities, and provide comparative analysis of method trade-offs in accuracy, cost, and scalability. Our findings offer insights for protocol designers reducing MEV leakage, validators optimizing transaction ordering, and analysts measuring ecosystem health through opportunity creation.

</details>


### [11] [Exploring Sparse Matrix Multiplication Kernels on the Cerebras CS-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27985)
*Milan Shah,Sheng Di,Michela Becchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次在Cerebras CS-3 AI加速器上系统研究了稀疏线性代数核心SpMM和SDDMM的性能表现，发现CS-3在中等稀疏度下相比CPU可获得20-100倍的加速，但在极高稀疏度下性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Cerebras CS-3在密集计算和大模型训练中表现出色，但其稀疏计算工作负载的潜力尚未被充分探索。许多重要应用（如图神经网络、线性求解器、推荐系统）都依赖稀疏线性代数核心，因此需要评估CS-3在这类稀疏工作负载上的性能表现。

Method: 针对稀疏-稠密矩阵乘法(SpMM)和采样稠密-稠密矩阵乘法(SDDMM)两个关键稀疏线性代数核心，设计了底层CS-3核心实现，并对设计方案进行优化以提高I/O性能、内存占用和大矩阵可扩展性。通过实验评估了内存占用以及相对于CPU的SpMM/SDDMM加速比。

Result: 实验表明：对于90%稀疏度的矩阵，CS-3在SpMM上的性能比CPU快100倍，且随着矩阵维度增加性能进一步提升；在SDDMM上比CPU快20倍。然而，当稀疏度超过99%时，CS-3出现性能下降，SpMM性能甚至低于CPU。

Conclusion: Cerebras CS-3数据流加速器在中等稀疏度的稀疏线性代数计算上具有显著优势，能够大幅超越传统CPU性能；但对于极高稀疏度的工作负载，其性能优势消失甚至出现劣势，这为未来稀疏计算加速器的设计优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: In recent years, novel AI accelerators have emerged as promising alternatives to GPU for AI model training and inference tasks. One such accelerator, the Cerebras CS-3, achieves strong performance on large model training as well as scientific applications like molecular dynamics simulations. While dense compute workloads have been thoroughly explored for the CS-3, its potential for sparse workloads has not been fully examined. Applications requiring sparse linear algebra kernels, such as GNNs, linear solvers, and recommendation systems, could achieve good performance on a dataflow accelerator like the CS-3.
  In this work, we explore two key sparse linear algebra kernels, sparse-dense matrix multiplication (SpMM) and sampled dense-dense matrix multiplication (SDDMM), on the Cerebras CS-3. We propose low-level CS-3 kernel designs for these operations and optimize our designs to improve I/O performance, memory footprint, and scalability to large matrices. Our evaluation examines memory footprint and SpMM/SDDMM speedup relative to CPU. The evaluation suggests that the CS-3 can outperform CPU by 100$\times$ for SpMM with 90\% sparse matrices with performance improving as sparse matrix dimensionality increases. SDDMM on CS-3 can outperform CPU 20$\times$ for 90\% sparse matrices. We additionally find that as sparsity increases to beyond 99\%, the CS-3 suffers from performance degradation that makes it slower than CPU for SpMM.

</details>


### [12] [From Impermanent Loss to Sustainable Gain: Quantifying Profitability Zones for Liquidity Providers on DEX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28014)
*Ignat Melnikov,Roman Vlasov,Vladimir Gorgadze,Andrey Seoev,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于自动做市商（AMM）的去中心化交易所交换协议，建立了套利者与流动性池交互的数学模型，推导了流动性提供者（LP）与套利者联合收益的边界，提出了预估非永久性损失（IL）发生所需区块数的方法，并得出了维持非永久性收益（IG）状态所需手续费的下界，为费率选择与风险评估提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融（DeFi）中的去中心化交易所（DEX）引入了新的市场低效性，易被套利者系统性利用，导致流动性提供者（LP）面临潜在损失。现有研究缺乏对套利者与AMM流动性池交互机制的深入量化分析，尤其缺乏关于LP风险与收益边界的理论框架。因此，本文旨在建立数学模型以分析二者交互，量化联合盈利区间，为费率设定与LP风险评估提供理论基础。

Method: 本文聚焦于基于自动做市商（AMM）且以储备乘积为不变量的交换协议，通过实证池配置数据，建立了套利者与流动性池交互的数学模型。在此基础上，推导LP与套利者联合收益的理论边界，提出基于模型参数估算非永久性损失（IL）发生所需区块数的方法，并计算在单区块内以特定概率维持在非永久性收益（IG）区域所需的最小池费率。

Result: 研究推导了LP与套利者联合收益的理论边界；提出了一种估计非永久性损失（IL）出现所需区块数的方法；确定了为达到特定概率维持在非永久性收益（IG）状态所需的手续费下界。该框架扩展了现有LP风险评估方法，量化了LP与套利者的共生盈利区间。

Conclusion: 本文提出的理论框架为DEX中手续费选择提供了原则性依据，有助于协调LP与套利者的激励一致性，进而增强市场稳定性，为DeFi生态系统的风险管理与机制设计提供了重要的理论支撑。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) is a rapidly evolving segment of blockchain technology that enables a transformative approach to financial services through Web3 applications. By leveraging smart contracts, DeFi allows developers to build flexible and innovative financial instruments. Among the most prominent DeFi primitives by liquidity are decentralized exchange~(DEX) swap protocols~(such as Uniswap, Curve, and Balancer) that facilitate fast token-to-token exchanges. However, new exchange mechanisms also introduce new market inefficiencies that can be systematically exploited by arbitrageurs. This paper focuses on swap protocols based on the Automated Market Maker~(AMM), where the product of reserves is preserved as an invariant. We analyze the interaction between arbitrageurs and AMM liquidity pools and develop a mathematical model grounded in empirical pool configurations. Using this model, we derive bounds on the joint revenue of liquidity providers~(LPs) and arbitrageurs, propose a method to estimate the expected number of blocks until the occurrence of Impermanent Loss~(IL), and obtain a lower bound on the pool fee required to achieve a fixed target probability of staying in the Impermanent Gain (IG) zone within a block. The proposed framework extends existing LP risk-assessment methodologies by quantifying symbiotic profitability zones, providing a principled basis for fee selection that aligns LP-arbitrageur incentives and enhances market stability.

</details>


### [13] [Characterizing Path-Independent Fees: A Route to Zero Impermanent Loss in CPMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28017)
*Andrey Voronin,Roman Vlasov,Vladimir Gorgadze,Andrey Seoev,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动做市商（AMM）中手续费再投资导致的路径依赖问题，提出了一类确保路径独立性的费用结构，并构建了能消除无常损失的参数化费用函数族，证明了不存在通用的零无常损失费用函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Uniswap V2及部分V4设计中，手续费自动再投资会导致交易最终状态依赖于交易拆分方式，这种路径依赖性增加了流动性提供者的风险评估难度，并影响了协议的可组合性保证。作者旨在解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 1) 刻画确保路径独立性的费用结构特征，证明联合费用因子必须仅依赖于当前池不变量k=xy；
2) 推导支配池动力学的常微分方程组并获得闭式积分交换公式；
3) 在该函数类中构建参数化费用函数族以实现零无常损失；
4) 通过受控仿真验证理论。

Result: 1) 成功表征了路径独立费用结构的充分必要条件；
2) 推导出池动力学的闭式解析解；
3) 构造出能在给定初始池状态下实现零无常损失的参数化费用函数族；
4) 证明了不存在能对所有初始状态同时消除无常损失的通用费用函数。

Conclusion: 本研究为协议设计者提供了费用优化的理论框架，能够在保持可组合性的同时协调流动性提供者与交易者的激励，为AMM协议设计提供了指导性原则。

Abstract: Constant Product Market Makers use fees that are typically fixed proportions of trade size. When these fees are automatically reinvested into the pool, as in Uniswap~V2 and some designs of Uniswap V4, the final state after a trade can depend on how the trade is split into smaller transactions. This path dependence complicates the risk assessment for liquidity providers and affects composability guarantees. We characterize the functional class of fee structures that ensure path independence: the combined fee factor must depend only on the current pool invariant k=xy. For this class, we derive a system of ordinary differential equations governing pool dynamics and obtain a closed-form integral exchange formula. Within this class, we construct a parametric family of fee functions that achieve zero Impermanent Loss for a given initial pool state, and prove that no universal fee function can eliminate Impermanent Loss for all initial states simultaneously. We analyze implications for arbitrage windows and slippage, and validate our theory through controlled simulations. Our framework provides protocol designers with a principled approach to fee optimization that aligns liquidity provider and trader incentives while preserving composability.

</details>


### [14] [Akita: A High Usability Simulation Framework for Computer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28073)
*Sabila Al Jannat,Ying Li,Mengyang He,Xuzhong Wang,Huizhi Zhao,Jingxiang Sun,Daoxuan Xu,Enze Xu,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Akita仿真引擎，通过将基础设施与硬件模型分离，解决了传统架构模拟器在易用性和开发体验上的痛点，实现了高性能并行仿真与便捷的监控可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机架构模拟器虽然功能强大，但在使用和开发上存在显著障碍，包括组件接口不规范、部署要求严格、性能优化（如并行化）需在组件层面手动管理以及监控可视化能力有限。作者指出，这些局限的根本原因在于社区长期忽视了用户和开发者体验，过分侧重于技术功能。

Method: 提出了Akita专用仿真引擎，其核心设计理念是将技术问题与用户体验解耦。关键技术包括：1) Smart Ticking与Availability Backpropagation机制，使开发者能编写简单的基于周期的代码，同时获得事件驱动仿真的性能优势；2) 透明的并行仿真机制，开发者只需编写单线程代码，Akita自动处理多核执行；3) 简单统一且强大的仿真追踪支持，实现实时监控与仿真后可视化。

Result: 通过案例研究验证了Akita的灵活性与有效性，包括开发基于 trace 的深度神经网络（DNN）仿真和 RISC-V CPU 仿真。实验表明，优先考虑开发者体验能够有效加速架构研究的进程。

Conclusion: 通过将仿真引擎基础设施与硬件模型分离，并系统性关注开发者体验，可以克服传统模拟器面临的根本性障碍，从而推动更高效的架构研究。Akita的成功实践证明了这一理念的可行性。

Abstract: Computer architecture simulation is essential for evaluating new designs without the need for costly tapeout. The community has developed dozens of valuable simulators that have enabled significant architectural advances. However, using and developing simulators remains a major barrier due to ad-hoc component interfaces, strict deployment requirements, the burden of managing performance optimizations like parallelization at the component level, and limited monitoring and visualization capabilities. The root cause of these limitations is the systematic neglect of user and developer experience in favor of technical functionality. We believe that only by separating technical concerns from user and developer experience concerns -- through a dedicated simulation engine decoupled from hardware models -- can the community overcome these fundamental obstacles and enable more productive architectural research.
  Akita embodies this philosophy as a dedicated simulation engine that cleanly separates infrastructure from architectural models. Smart Ticking and Availability Backpropagation let developers write simple cycle-based code while achieving event-driven performance. Parallel simulation happens transparently -- developers write single-threaded code while Akita handles multi-core execution. Akita's simple, uniform, yet powerful simulation tracing support enables real-time monitoring and post-simulation visualization. We demonstrate the flexibility of Akita through case studies, including the development of a trace-based DNN simulation and a RISC-V CPU simulation, showing how prioritizing developer experience accelerates architectural research.

</details>


### [15] [Agentic Compilation: Mitigating the LLM Rerun Crisis for Minimized-Inference-Cost Web Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09718)
*Jagadeesh Chundru*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对LLM驱动Web代理在重复任务中面临的“重运行危机”（成本和延迟随执行次数线性增长），提出编译-执行架构。通过一次性LLM调用生成确定性JSON工作流蓝图，由轻量级运行时驱动浏览器执行，将推理成本从O(M×N)降至分摊O(1)。实验显示零样本编译成功率80-94%，经少量人工修补可达近100%，单次编译成本仅0.002-0.092美元，实现了大规模自动化在经济上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连续推理循环的LLM Web代理存在根本的可扩展性约束：对于重复性任务，代币消耗和API延迟随执行频率线性增长。例如，一个5步工作流执行500次，连续代理的推理成本高达约150美元，即使采用激进缓存仍需约15美元，这严重限制了大规模自动化的经济可行性。

Method: 提出编译-执行架构，将LLM推理与浏览器执行解耦：1）DOM清理模块（DSM）生成高效令牌的语义表示；2）一次性LLM调用处理该表示并输出确定性JSON工作流蓝图；3）轻量级运行时无需进一步模型查询即可驱动浏览器执行。该架构将推理复杂度从O(M×N)降至分摊O(1)，其中M为重运行次数，N为序列动作数。JSON中间表示的模块化设计支持最小化人在回路（HITL）修补。

Result: 在数据提取、表单填写和指纹识别任务上的实证评估显示：零样本编译成功率达80-94%；通过最小化人工修补可将执行可靠性提升至近100%；跨五个前沿模型的单次编译成本在0.002-0.092美元之间，相比连续架构的150美元（无缓存）或15美元（激进缓存）实现数量级降低。

Conclusion: 确定性编译范式成功解决了连续推理架构的 scalability 约束，使之前在经济上不可行的大规模自动化成为可能。编译-执行架构通过将推理成本从线性增长转为固定成本，结合模块化设计支持的高效人工修补，为LLM驱动的Web自动化提供了切实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-driven web agents operating through continuous inference loops -- repeatedly querying a model to evaluate browser state and select actions -- exhibit a fundamental scalability constraint for repetitive tasks. We characterize this as the Rerun Crisis: the linear growth of token expenditure and API latency relative to execution frequency. For a 5-step workflow over 500 iterations, a continuous agent incurs approximately 150.00 USD in inference costs; even with aggressive caching, this remains near 15.00 USD. We propose a Compile-and-Execute architecture that decouples LLM reasoning from browser execution, reducing per-workflow inference cost to under 0.10 USD. A one-shot LLM invocation processes a token-efficient semantic representation from a DOM Sanitization Module (DSM) and emits a deterministic JSON workflow blueprint. A lightweight runtime then drives the browser without further model queries. We formalize this cost reduction from O(M x N) to amortized O(1) inference scaling, where M is the number of reruns and N is the sequential actions. Empirical evaluation across data extraction, form filling, and fingerprinting tasks yields zero-shot compilation success rates of 80-94%. Crucially, the modularity of the JSON intermediate representation allows minimal Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) patching to elevate execution reliability to near-100%. At per-compilation costs between 0.002 USD and 0.092 USD across five frontier models, these results establish deterministic compilation as a paradigm enabling economically viable automation at scales previously infeasible under continuous architectures.

</details>

### [16] [A High-Throughput Compute-Efficient POMDP Hide-And-Seek-Engine (HASE) for Multi-Agent Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27162)
*Timothy Flavin,Sandip Sen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hide-And-Seek-Engine，一个用C++原生架构的高计算效率Dec-POMDP引擎，通过数据导向设计、缓存行对齐和零拷贝PyTorch内存桥接技术，在单代理场景下实现了高达3300万步/秒的吞吐量，比基线NumPy实现提升约3500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习算法在应用于去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDPs）时存在高样本复杂度问题，现有项目（如SampleFactory、EnvPool、Brax和IsaacLab）通过将环境并行执行迁移至C++线程池或GPU来降低计算成本，本文旨在优化人机协同决策层面的计算效率。

Method: 采用数据导向设计（DOD）原则，实现显式64字节缓存行对齐以消除伪共享，并利用固定内存和直接内存访问（DMA）技术构建零拷贝PyTorch内存桥，在AMD Ryzen 9950X（16核）处理器上实现了单代理、1024环境、去中心化观测的高效执行。

Result: 引擎在单代理场景下实现了高达33,000,000步/秒（SPS）的吞吐量；十代理场景下降至700万SPS（其中随机动作生成占总运行时间的1/3）；相比基线单线程向量化NumPy实现，吞吐量提升约3500倍；成功通过PPO、DQN和SAC算法在数分钟内训练出协作多智能体策略。

Conclusion: Hide-And-Seek-Engine成功验证了其高性能和通用性，为人机协同决策优化提供了一个计算高效的Dec-POMDP解决方案，能够显著加速多智能体强化学习训练过程。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms exhibit high sample complexity, particularly when applied to Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). As a response, projects such as SampleFactory, EnvPool, Brax, and IsaacLab migrate parallel execution of classic environments such as MuJoCo and Atari into C++ thread pools or the GPU to decrease the computational cost of environment steps. We are interested in optimizing the decision-level of human-AI joint operations, so we introduce a compute-efficient Dec-POMDP engine natively architected in C++ called Hide-And-Seek-Engine. By employing Data-Oriented Design (DOD) principles, explicit 64-byte cache-line alignment to remove false sharing, and a zero-copy PyTorch memory bridge using pinned memory and Direct Memory Access (DMA), our engine sustains throughput of up to 33,000,000 steps per second (SPS) in a single-agent, 1024-environment, decentralized observations on an AMD Ryzen 9950X (16 cores). Ten agents reduces FPS to 7M SPS with generating random actions contributing 1/3rd the total runtime for reference. The engine achieves a throughput increase of approximately 3,500$\times$ over the baseline single threaded vectorized NumPy implementation and successfully trains cooperative multi-agent policies via PPO, DQN, and SAC in minutes, validating both its performance and generality.

</details>

### [17] [Towards Topology-Aware Very Large-Scale Photonic AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26966)
*Belal Jahannia,Abdolah Amirany,Hamed Dalir*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化的光子加速器架构，通过系统级分析揭示了由网格拓扑主导的“利用率墙”瓶颈，并证明对称拓扑相比线性配置可显著提升利用率和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的发展使电子加速器面临“存储墙”瓶颈，而现有光子加速器研究多集中于器件层面，缺乏对系统级可扩展性的深入探索。

Method: 提出了一种基于4×4光子张量核心单元的模块化扩展光子加速器架构；在架构分析中综合考虑了插入损耗、扇出惩罚和激光功率限制等光子硬件的实际扩展约束，并使用多达1024个处理元件在多种DNN工作负载上进行评估。

Result: 识别出光子领域特有的拓扑主导扩展瓶颈——“利用率墙”，即性能受限于网格拓扑而非硬件尺寸；提出了“对称网格规则”，证明对称拓扑相比线性配置可将利用率提升最高6倍，并减少超过40%的内存访问。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的扩展策略对于实现高能效、高性能的光子AI加速器至关重要，系统级架构设计应优先考虑对称拓扑以突破扩展瓶颈。

Abstract: The rapid growth of deep neural networks (DNNs) has exposed fundamental limitations in electronic accelerators, where data movement dominates energy consumption, commonly referred to as the memory wall. Photonic accelerators offer a compelling alternative due to their inherent parallelism and high-speed matrix operations. However, existing research largely focuses on device-level innovations, leaving system-level scalability insufficiently explored. In this paper, we present a scalable photonic accelerator architecture based on a modular scale-out paradigm using 4 X 4 photonic tensor core units. We perform a systematic architectural analysis that incorporates the practical scaling limits of photonic hardware, including insertion loss, fanout penalties, and laser power limits, which restrict monolithic photonic scaling. Through evaluation on representative DNN workloads (GoogleNet, ResNet-18, MobileNet, and AlphaGo Zero) with up to 1024 processing elements, we identify a topology-dominated scaling bottleneck in the photonic domain, termed the Utilization Wall, where performance is governed by grid topology rather than hardware size. We further establish the Symmetric Grid Rule, demonstrating that symmetric topologies improve utilization by up to 6X while reducing memory access by over 40% compared to linear configurations, which reveal that topology-aware scaling is essential for achieving energy-efficient and high-performance photonic AI accelerators.

</details>


### [18] [Predictive Multi-Tier Memory Management for KV Cache in Large-Scale GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26968)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的KV缓存管理系统，通过架构感知的内存 sizing、六级存储层次扩展和贝叶斯重用预测器，解决大模型推理服务中的内存瓶颈，实现1.7-2.9倍吞吐提升和47%成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型GPU推理服务系统存在三大效率问题：（1）缺乏跨注意力架构（特别是MLA）的统一KV缓存大小计算，导致高达57倍内存过度配置；（2）KV缓存局限于单一GPU HBM存储层，未利用CPU DRAM、CXL、NVMe等丰富存储层次；（3）响应式驱逐策略丢弃可重用状态，迫使冗余重计算。

Method: 提出三个核心技术：（1）架构变体感知的sizing引擎，精确计算各注意力类型的内存需求；（2）六级存储层次架构，将单节点有效KV缓存容量从40GB扩展至38TB以上，同时保持热条目亚毫秒TTFT；（3）贝叶斯重用预测器，基于Beta共轭先验对16个(块类型,转移类型)对建模，结合EMA评分的头部粒度驱逐和RoPE感知预取。

Result: 在ShareGPT、LMSYS-Chat-1M和智能体工作负载的追踪回放验证中，实现70-84%缓存命中率。分析性预测显示：相比SOTA基线，TTFT降低1.4-2.1倍，吞吐提升1.7-2.9倍，成本降低47%，批量大小提升达7.4倍。

Conclusion: 该统一系统有效解决了KV缓存内存管理的三大瓶颈，在保持低延迟的同时显著提升了大规模GPU推理服务的吞吐量和成本效率，为大模型部署提供了实用的优化方案。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache memory management is the primary bottleneck limiting throughput and cost-efficiency in large-scale GPU inference serving. Current systems suffer from three compounding inefficiencies: (1) the absence of unified KV cache sizing across all attention architectures--particularly multi-head latent attention (MLA), which is unsupported in general-purpose frameworks, resulting in up to 57x memory over-provisioning; (2) confinement of KV cache to a single memory tier (GPU HBM) despite the availability of a rich hierarchy spanning CPU DRAM, CXL-attached memory, NVMe via GPUDirect Storage, RDMA fabric, and parallel filesystems; and (3) reactive eviction policies that discard reusable state, forcing redundant recomputation. We present a unified system that addresses all three problems. Our architecture-variant-aware sizing engine computes exact memory requirements per attention type, enabling up to 7.4x higher batch sizes. A six-tier memory hierarchy extends effective KV cache capacity from 40 GB to over 38 TB per node while maintaining sub-millisecond time-to-first-token (TTFT) for hot entries. A Bayesian reuse predictor with Beta conjugate priors over 16 (block-type, transition-type) pairs achieves 70-84% cache hit rates, combined with EMA-scored head-granular eviction and RoPE-aware prefetching. Component-level validation on trace replay using ShareGPT, LMSYS-Chat-1M, and agentic workloads demonstrates 70-84% cache hit rates. Analytical projections combining validated component behavior with published hardware specifications indicate 1.4-2.1x projected TTFT reduction, 1.7-2.9x throughput improvement, and 47% cost reduction compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [19] [Multibit neural inference in a N-ary crossbar architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26979)
*Anatole Moureaux,Anthony Lopes Temporao,Flavio Abreu Araujo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对N进制存内计算(CIM)交叉阵列的仿真框架，在4x4四态磁性隧道结(MTJ)阵列上实现了MNIST分类精度94.48%，并通过分析确定了最佳单元状态数以平衡量化误差与电阻态分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存内计算(CIM)通过在存储器交叉阵列中执行模拟矩阵向量乘法(MVM)来实现高效的神经网络推理。然而，硬件实现中的非理想因素会导致性能下降。因此需要开发一个仿真框架来评估和分析这些误差来源及其影响，以优化系统设计。

Method: 提出了一个针对N进制交叉阵列架构的仿真框架，仅需最小化实现假设即可检索MVM结果。使用(4x4)四态磁性隧道结(MTJ)阵列进行XOR和MNIST分类任务验证。采用PCA降维技术减小软硬件性能差距，并系统性地研究了权重量化、系统性非理想因素和随机噪声的影响。

Result: 在MNIST分类任务上达到94.48%的精度(软件基准为97.56%)。PCA降维有效减小了软硬件性能差距。确定了权重量化是主要误差源。发现由于阵列中的平均效应，单元特定的随机噪声比系统性误差的危害更小。确定了最佳单元状态数，能平衡量化误差与电阻态分辨率，从而最小化总MVM误差。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架能够有效评估N进制存内计算架构的性能。系统性误差比随机噪声更具破坏性。存在一个最优的单元状态数配置，可以在量化精度和硬件实现难度之间取得最佳平衡，为存内计算硬件设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: In-memory computing (IMC) enables energy-efficient neural network inference by computing analog matrix-vector multiplications (MVM) in memory crossbar arrays. In this work we present a simulation framework for N-ary crossbar architectures that retrieves MVM results with minimal implementation assumptions. The XOR and MNIST classification tasks were successfully inferred using a simulated crossbar array of (4x4) 4-states magnetic tunnel junctions (MTJ). MNIST accuracy reached 94.48% (vs. 97.56% software baseline). The software-hardware performance gap was further reduced using PCA dimensionality reduction. We identified weight quantization as the primary error source, and studied its impact alongside systematic nonidealities and random noise. We find that cell-specific random noise is less detrimental than systematic errors due to averaging across the array. Finally, we demonstrate an optimal number of states per cell that balances quantization error against resistance state resolution to minimize total MVM error.

</details>


### [20] [EMiX: Emulating Beyond Single-FPGA Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27012)
*Alexander Kropotov,Miquel Moreto,Behzad Salami*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EMiX，一个可扩展的多FPGA框架，通过分布式仿真打破了单FPGA资源限制，成功在8个FPGA上实现了64核RISC-V架构的全系统仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模多核系统设计规模的增长，传统的单FPGA仿真方法已无法满足硬件资源需求，限制了全系统仿真的可行性。因此，迫切需要一种能够突破单FPGA资源限制的可扩展仿真方案。

Method: 提出了EMiX多FPGA框架，采用系统化的方法将整体多核设计划分为多个组件，并将这些组件部署在多个互联的FPGA上。该方法通过有效利用FPGA间互连来平衡可扩展性与性能，且无需对底层RTL设计进行根本性重构。

Result: 在8个互联的Alveo U55c FPGA上成功构建了64核架构的原型（核心数和FPGA数量可扩展），并实现了包括Linux启动在内的全系统执行，验证了该框架的可行性。

Conclusion: EMiX证明了通过多FPGA分布式架构可以有效解决大规模多核系统仿真中的资源瓶颈问题，为超大规模芯片设计验证提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，并将作为开源平台发布以促进社区发展。

Abstract: FPGA-level emulation is a key step in pre-silicon chip design validation. However, emulating large-scale multi-core systems increasingly exceed the hardware resource capacity of a single FPGA, limiting the feasibility of full-system emulation. To address this challenge, we introduce EMiX, a scalable multi-FPGA framework that enables distributed emulation of multi-core RISC-V architectures beyond single-FPGA resource limits. EMiX systematically partitions a monolithic multi-core design into multiple components and deploys them across multiple interconnected FPGAs, effectively exploiting inter-FPGA interconnects to balance scalability and performance without requiring fundamental RTL redesign. We prototype EMiX with a 64-core architecture across eight interconnected Alveo U55c FPGAs (scalable on core and FPGA counts), successfully demonstrating full-system execution including Linux boot. EMiX will be released as an open-source platform.

</details>


### [21] [Verification and Validation (V&V)-in-the-Loop for RISC-V Design: The Holistic Vision of BZL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27013)
*Sajjad Ahmed,Alexander Kropotov,Roberto Ignacio Genovese,Bernat Homs,Eloi Merino,Francesco Urbani,Henrique Yano,Iván Díaz,Joan Gracia Fernandez,Matteo Toselli,Muhammad Imran,Muhammad Abu Bakar Umar Haider Iqbal,Nadeem Yaseen,Quswar Abid,Shaista Cheema,Samuel Sanchez,Daniel Garcia,Joan Cabré,Mostafa Elyasi,Fernando Ayats,Miquel Moreto,Teresa Cervero,Oscar Palomar,Behzad Salami*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Barcelona Zetascale Lab (BZL)项目提出的一种面向RISC-V高性能计算芯片的整体预硅验证与确认(V&V)方法，集成了UVM验证环境、FPGA验证平台和CI/CD流程，以确保芯片设计的功能正确性和系统级验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加强欧洲在基于RISC-V的高性能计算芯片设计与制造方面的能力，需要一种高度可靠的预硅验证与确认方法，以确保RISC-V芯片设计在流片前的功能正确性和系统级可靠性。

Method: 提出了一种整体预硅验证与确认(V&V)方法，集成三个互补平台：(1)基于UVM的验证环境，用于全面验证RTL功能；(2)基于FPGA的验证平台，实现系统级预硅硬件-软件RTL验证；(3)CI/CD流程，持续自动化跨领域的构建、部署和测试。将这些平台嵌入工业级V&V循环中，并利用大规模CPU和FPGA硬件基础设施。

Result: BZL的V&V流程为RISC-V芯片设计的预硅功能正确性和系统级验证提供了稳健且可扩展的基础，能够支持可靠硬件开发和软件集成的持续演进。

Conclusion: 该V&V流程可作为欧洲战略倡议（如EPI和DARE）及其他地区类似项目的关键推动力，为RISC-V芯片设计的预硅验证提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The Barcelona Zetascale Lab (BZL) project aims to strengthening Europe's capacity in the design and manufacture of RISC-V based high-performance computing chips. In this context, we present a holistic pre-silicon verification and validation (V&V) methodology targeting highly robust RISC-V chip designs. This paper provides an overview of BZL's V&V approach, which integrates three complementary platforms: (1) a UVM-based verification environment to thoroughly validate RTL functionality; (2) an FPGA-based validation platform that enables system-level pre-silicon hardware-software RTL validation; and (3) a CI/CD flow that continuously automates build, deployment, and tests across these domains. By embedding these platforms into an industrial-grade V&V loop and exploiting large-scale CPU and FPGA hardware infrastructures, the BZL project enables continuous evolution of reliable hardware development and software integration. We believe that the BZL's V&V flow represents a robust and scalable foundation for ensuring the pre-silicon functional correctness and system level validation of RISC-V chip designs, and can serve as a key enabler for strategic initiatives in Europe, such as EPI and DARE, and beyond.

</details>


### [22] [RCW-CIM: A Digital CIM-based LLM Accelerator with Read-Compute/Write](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27384)
*Yan-Cheng Guo,Tian-Sheuan Chang,Jian-Wei Su*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出RCW架构与非线性算子融合机制，有效解决了DCIM在LLM加速中的权重更新延迟问题，显著降低了计算延迟并提升了能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数字计算存储(DCIM)架构在加速大语言模型(LLM)时，往往忽视了权重更新延迟问题。由于LLM的权重规模远超单个CIM宏的容量，这一延迟问题变得至关重要。

Method: 提出读-计算/写(RCW)架构以最小化权重更新延迟，并引入非线性算子融合机制缓解依赖引起的延迟；同时设计权重驻留-输出列驻留(WS-OCS)数据流，减少外部DRAM访问和内部CIM权重更新。

Result: 在Llama2-7B模型上，RCW将解码计算延迟降低21.59%；非线性算子融合机制实现69.17%的延迟降低；WS-OCS数据流在1024 tokens预填充阶段分别减少51.6%的外部DRAM访问和87.6%的内部CIM权重更新，总体延迟降低49.76%。芯片采用TSMC 22nm工艺，100MHz工作频率，实现3.28 TOPS和42.3 TOPS/W的性能，INT4权重Llama2模型的预填充延迟为4.2ms，解码速度为26.87 tokens/s。

Conclusion: 所提出的RCW-CIM架构通过硬件架构优化、算子融合和数据流设计三方面的协同创新，有效解决了LLM加速中的权重更新瓶颈，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了性能和能效，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了可行的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Digital computing-in-memory (DCIM) has emerged as a promising solution for large language model (LLM) acceleration by minimizing data transfers between external DRAM and on-chip accelerators while maintaining high precision for superior accuracy. However, existing CIM architectures often overlook weight update latency, which becomes critical as LLM weights are far larger than a single CIM macro capacity. To address this issue, this paper proposes a read-compute/write (RCW) architecture that effectively minimizes weight update latency, along with a nonlinear operator fusion that further mitigates dependencyinduced latency. The proposed RCW reduces decoding computing latency by 21.59% on the Llama2-7B model. In addition, the nonlinear operator fusion mechanism achieves a 69.17% latency reduction through efficient partial accumulation and group-based approximation. Furthermore, a weight-stationary and output column stationary (WS-OCS) dataflow is introduced to reduce both external DRAM access and internal CIM weight updates by 51.6% and 87.6% respectively during the prefill phase of 1024 tokens, leading to an overall 49.76% latency reduction. Fabricated using TSMC 22 nm CMOS technology and operating at 100 MHz, the proposed RCW-CIM achieves 3.28 TOPS and 42.3 TOPS/W, enabling 4.2 ms prefill latency and 26.87 decoded tokens per second for the INT4-weight Llama2 model with dual DDR5-6400 memory.

</details>


### [23] [VitaLLM: A Versatile, Ultra-Compact Ternary LLM Accelerator with Dependency-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27396)
*Zi-Wei Lin,Tian-Sheuan Chang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VitaLLM，一种面向三值化大语言模型推理的软硬件协同设计加速器，通过异构双核计算策略、前导1预测机制和依赖感知调度框架，在TSMC 16nm工艺下实现了17.4 TOPS/mm²/W的优异性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署大语言模型面临内存带宽和功耗的关键瓶颈。虽然三值量化（如BitNet b1.58）能显著减少模型尺寸，但在通用硬件上的直接部署仍受限于工作负载不平衡、带宽受限的解码阶段以及严格的数据依赖问题。

Method: 提出VitaLLM加速器，采用异构双核计算策略：专用的TINT-Cores处理大规模三值投影，统一的BoothFlex-Core处理混合精度注意力机制，确保计算密集的预填充阶段和带宽受限的解码阶段均能高效利用。同时开发前导1预测（LOP）机制剪枝冗余的KV缓存读取，并设计依赖感知调度框架隐藏非线性操作的延迟。此外，还探索了扩展的位串行设计（BoothFlex-BS）以支持精度敏捷推理。

Result: 在TSMC 16nm工艺下实现，解码吞吐量达70.70 tokens/s，面积仅0.223 mm²，功耗65.97 mW，实现了17.4 TOPS/mm²/W的优异性能指标，显著超越现有最先进加速器。

Conclusion: VitaLLM通过软硬件协同设计和异构计算架构有效解决了三值LLM部署的关键挑战，为边缘设备上的高效大语言模型推理提供了可行的解决方案，同时其架构具有良好的适应性，可扩展支持精度敏捷推理。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices faces critical bottlenecks in memory bandwidth and power consumption. While ternary quantization (e.g., BitNet b1.58) significantly reduces model size, its direct deployment on general-purpose hardware is hindered by workload imbalance, bandwidth-bound decoding, and strict data dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{VitaLLM}, a hardware-software co-designed accelerator tailored for efficient ternary LLM inference. We introduce a heterogeneous \textbf{Dual-Core Compute Strategy} that synergizes specialized TINT-Cores for massive ternary projections with a unified BoothFlex-Core for mixed-precision attention, ensuring high utilization across both compute-bound prefill and bandwidth-bound decode stages. Furthermore, we develop a \textbf{Leading One Prediction (LOP)} mechanism to prune redundant Key-Value (KV) cache fetches and a \textbf{Dependency-Aware Scheduling} framework to hide the latency of nonlinear operations. Implemented in TSMC 16nm technology, VitaLLM achieves a decoding throughput of 70.70 tokens/s within an ultra-compact area of 0.223 mm$^2$ and a power consumption of 65.97 mW. The design delivers a superior Figure of Merit (FOM) of 17.4 TOPS/mm$^2$/W, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art accelerators. Finally, we explore an extended bit-serial design (BoothFlex-BS) to demonstrate the architecture's adaptability for precision-agile inference.

</details>


### [24] [CuLifter: Lifting GPU Binaries to Typed IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27486)
*Jisheng Zhao,Huanzhi Pu,Shinnung Jeong,Chihyo Ahn,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CuLifter，一个针对GPU二进制（SASS）的LLVM IR提升框架，通过基于冲突检测的约束传播技术解决寄存器类型恢复这一核心难题，在大型数据集上实现了99.98%的函数提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU编译器将所有数据类型统一到单一的寄存器文件中，导致类型信息丢失，这使得依赖类型信息的二进制分析工具无法正常工作。本文旨在解决GPU二进制提升中类型恢复这一核心挑战。

Method: 提出CuLifter框架，采用基于冲突检测的约束传播方法来恢复寄存器类型，同时重构显式控制流并聚合多指令模式，将NVIDIA SASS汇编指令提升为LLVM中间表示（IR）。

Result: 在涵盖开源应用、厂商库和优化ML运行时的8个基准测试套件（919个cubin文件中的24,437个GPU函数）上，CuLifter成功将99.98%的函数提升为有效的LLVM IR。消融实验表明，类型恢复是产生语义正确IR的唯一关键步骤：禁用类型恢复会导致x86通过率从73.8%骤降至0%。

Conclusion: 类型恢复是GPU二进制提升的核心挑战，通过有效的类型恢复机制，可以实现对GPU二进制代码的大规模、高成功率的语义正确提升，为后续的二进制分析和优化奠定基础。

Abstract: GPU compilers merge all data types into a single unified register file, erasing the type information that binary-analysis tools rely on. We show that type recovery from this untyped register file is the central challenge of GPU binary lifting. We present CuLifter, a SASS-to-LLVM IR lifting framework that recovers register types via constraint propagation with conflict detection, reconstructs explicit control flow, and aggregates multi-instruction patterns. Across eight benchmark suites (24,437 GPU functions in 919 cubins) spanning open-source applications, vendor libraries, and optimized ML runtimes, CuLifter successfully lifts 99.98% of functions to valid LLVM IR. An ablation study confirms that type recovery is the only step required to produce semantically correct IR: disabling it drops the x86 pass rate from 73.8% to 0%, a 73.8 percentage-point drop.

</details>


### [25] [HAVEN: Hybrid Automated Verification ENgine for UVM Testbench Synthesis with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27643)
*Chang-Chih Meng,Yu-Ren Lu,Guan-Yu Lin,Tsung Tai Yeh,Kai-Chiang Wu,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HAVEN系统，通过避免LLM直接编写HDL代码，利用模板引擎和协议感知DSL生成UVM测试平台和序列，在19个开源IP设计上实现了100%编译成功率、90.6%代码覆盖率和87.9%功能覆盖率，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC验证占开发周期近70%，现有基于LLM的方法因HDL训练数据稀缺而难以正确生成测试平台代码，亟需新的自动化验证方法来克服LLM在硬件描述语言生成方面的局限性。

Method: HAVEN采用混合方法：1) 利用LLM代理分析设计规格生成结构化架构计划，通过预定义的协议特定Jinja2模板生成所有UVM组件；2) 引入协议感知序列DSL将序列分解为细粒度步骤类型，先使用预定义DSL模式建立高覆盖率序列，再迭代利用LLM分析覆盖率缺口并生成针对性DSL序列。

Result: 在涵盖三种接口协议（Direct、Wishbone、AXI4-Lite）的19个开源IP设计上，HAVEN实现了100%编译成功率、平均90.6%代码覆盖率、87.9%功能覆盖率，在LLM辅助测试平台生成系统中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: HAVEN是首个利用预定义协议特定模板和协议感知DSL生成完整UVM组件及序列的系统，通过避免LLM直接编写HDL代码有效解决了验证自动化难题，为IC验证流程提供了可靠的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Integrated Circuit (IC) verification consumes nearly 70% of the IC development cycle, and recent research leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate testbenches and reduce verification overhead. However, LLMs have difficulty generating testbenches correctly. Unlike high-level programming languages, Hardware Description Languages (HDLs) are extremely rare in LLMs training data, leading LLMs to produce incorrect code. To overcome challenges when using LLMs to generate Universal Verification Methodology (UVM) testbenches and sequences, wepropose HAVEN (Hybrid Automated Verification ENgine) to prevent LLMs from writing HDL directly. For UVM testbench generation, HAVEN utilizes LLM agents to analyze design specifications to produce a structured architectural plan. The HAVEN Template Engine then combines with predefined and protocol-specific templates to generate all UVM components with correct bus-handshake timing. For UVM sequence generation, HAVEN introduces a Protocol-Aware Sequence Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that decomposes sequences into fine-grained step types. A set of predefined DSL patterns first establishes sequences that achieve a high coverage rate without LLM involvement. HAVEN continues to improve the coverage rate by iteratively leveraging LLM agents to analyze coverage gap reports and compose additional targeted DSL sequences. Unlike previous works, HAVEN is the first system that utilizes pre-defined, protocol-specific Jinja2 templates to generate all UVM components and UVM sequences using our proposed Protocol-Aware DSL and rule-based code generator. Our experimental results on 19 open-source IP designs spanning three interface protocols (Direct, Wishbone, AXI4-Lite) show that HAVEN achieves 100% compilation success, 90.6% code coverage, and 87.9% functional coverage on average, and is SOTA among LLM-assisted testbench generation systems.

</details>


### [26] [RuC: HDL-Agnostic Rule Completion Benchmark Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27780)
*Arnau Ayguadé Domingo,Miquel Alberti-Binimelis,Cristian Gutierrez-Gomez,Emanuele Parisi,Razine Moundir Ghorab,Miquel Moreto,Gokcen Kestor,Dario Garcia-Gasulla*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了RuC框架，一个语法驱动的基准生成器，用于生成可控粒度的RTL代码补全任务，并通过实验发现FIM提示策略效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代码补全基准要么评估整个硬件模块生成，要么评估单行补全，缺乏对补全样本粒度和语法范围的控制能力。

Method: 提出了RuC（Rule Completion）框架，利用目标硬件描述语言（HDL）语法自动生成代码补全任务。该框架通过语法规则掩码代码区域，并提示模型利用周围未掩码代码作为上下文重新生成被掩码部分，从而实现可控且可扩展的评估。

Result: 利用RuC生成了基于Tiny Tapeout TT07和CVE2 RISC-V核心的两个SystemVerilog基准，并对现代开源LLM进行了比较研究。结果表明补全性能高度依赖于模型类型、掩码区域的语法结构和提示策略，其中Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM)提示策略得分最高。

Conclusion: 语法驱动、任意粒度的基准测试对于有意义的评估LLM在RTL开发工作流中的能力具有重要价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly improved in performance across code-related tasks, making their integration into Register Transfer Level (RTL) development increasingly attractive. Mimicking the behavior of inline code assistants, many benchmarks evaluate LLMs' capabilities in code completion, either assessing the generation of entire hardware modules or the completion of a single line within a module. However both of these approaches lack the ability to control the granularity of the code-completion sample size and the syntactic range of completions. To overcome these limitations, we present a framework for language-agnostic rule completion (RuC), a grammar-driven, rule-selectable benchmark generator that automatically produces RTL code-completion tasks from a set of input hardware description sources. RuC uses the target Hardware Description Language (HDL) grammar to mask syntactically defined code regions and prompts a model to regenerate them using the surrounding unmasked code as context, enabling a controlled and scalable evaluation of the domain-specific model's code-understanding capabilities, ranging from assignments to the reconstruction of entire logic blocks. We use RuC to generate two SystemVerilog rule-completion benchmarks from the Tiny Tapeout shuttle TT07 and the CVE2 RISC-V core to demonstrate RuC's applicability to a broad range of designs, and conduct a comparative study of the code completion capabilities of modern open-source LLMs across diverse settings. Results indicate that completion performance strongly depends on the model type, the grammatical structure of the masked region, and the prompting strategy. Specifically, the highest scores are obtained with Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) prompting. These findings highlight the value of grammar-driven, arbitrarily granular benchmarks for meaningful evaluation of LLM capabilities in RTL development workflows.

</details>


### [27] [AME-PIM: Can Memory be Your Next Tensor Accelerator?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27808)
*Emanuele Venieri,Simone Manoni,Alberto Florian,Jaehyun Park,Kyomin Sohn,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将RISC-V AME矩阵扩展映射到HBM-PIM的执行模型，通过无归约外积数据流实现了在存内计算中完全无需主机干预的矩阵运算加速，单通道性能达14.9 GFLOP/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用HBM-PIM平台指令集有限且需要专用软件栈，本研究旨在探索HBM-PIM能否作为ISA级矩阵加速的后端，以RISC-V AME扩展为语义参考，解决数据移动开销问题。

Method: 提出基于PEP的执行模型，将AME元素级和矩阵指令映射到HBM-PIM微内核；创新性地引入无归约外积数据流，克服HBM-PIM缺乏原生归约支持的限制，实现存内完全累加。

Result: 在Samsung Aquabolt-XL平台上实验表明，AME矩阵分块乘法在单个HBM伪通道上达到14.9 GFLOP/s（59.4 FLOP/周期），支持元素级运算、GEMV和GEMM的端到端PIM模式执行。

Conclusion: HBM-PIM可作为ISA级矩阵加速的有效后端，所提方案成功实现了最小化主机参与和片外传输，为存内计算在矩阵运算领域的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: High Bandwidth Memory with Processing-in-Memory (HBM-PIM) offers an opportunity to reduce data movement by executing computation directly inside memory, but current commercial platforms expose limited instruction sets and require specialized software stacks. In this work, we investigate whether HBM-PIM can serve as a backend for ISA-level matrix acceleration, using the RISC-V Attached Matrix Extension (AME) as a semantic reference. We propose a PEP-based execution model that maps AME element-wise and matrix instructions to HBM-PIM micro-kernels and data instructions in memory operations. Differently from SoA HBM-PIM, we introduce a reduction-free outer-product dataflow that enables accumulation entirely within memory despite the lack of native reduction support. Our approach supports end-to-end execution of element-wise operations, GEMV, and GEMM in PIM mode, minimizing host involvement and off-chip transfers. An experimental evaluation on Samsung Aquabolt-XL shows that AME matrix tile multiplication achieves up to 14.9 GFLOP/s (59.4 FLOP/cycle) on a single HBM pseudo-channel.

</details>


### [28] [NeuroRing: Scaling Spiking Neural Networks via Multi-FPGA Bidirectional Ring Topologies and Stream-Dataflow Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28059)
*Muhammad Ihsan Al Hafiz,Artur Podobas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了NeuroRing，一种基于FPGA的模块化、可扩展的脉冲神经网络加速器，通过流数据流架构和双向环形拓扑，实现了大规模SNN的快速实时执行和良好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络(SNN)是能效高的事件驱动计算范式，但大规模SNN执行面临挑战，因为稀疏脉冲通信和同步会主导运行时间。现有的CPU、GPU、ASIC和FPGA解决方案在可编程性、效率和可扩展性之间存在不同的权衡。

Method: 提出NeuroRing，一种基于流数据流架构和双向环形拓扑的模块化可扩展SNN加速器，使用高层次综合(HLS)在可编程FPGA上实现。支持模块化的单FPGA和多FPGA部署，并通过与NEST模拟器集成兼容现有的SNN工作流程。

Result: 在皮层微电路基准测试和数独约束满足工作负载上评估NeuroRing。结果表明：NeuroRing保持了NEST参考模型的关键活动统计数据；实现了全规模皮层微电路的超实时执行，实时因子(RTF)为0.83；展现出有意义的强扩展性和弱扩展性；在两个可编程FPGA上提供了具有竞争力的能效。

Conclusion: NeuroRing定位为神经科学模拟和更广泛事件驱动应用的灵活可扩展平台，填补了现有解决方案在可编程性、效率和可扩展性之间的差距。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are a promising paradigm for energy-efficient event-driven computation, but large-scale SNN execution remains challenging because sparse spike communication and synchronization can dominate runtime. Existing solutions across CPU, GPU, ASIC, and FPGA platforms offer different trade-offs between programmability, efficiency, and scalability. To address this gap, we present NeuroRing, a modular and scalable SNN accelerator based on a stream-dataflow architecture and a bidirectional ring topology, implemented in High-Level Synthesis (HLS) on programmable FPGAs. NeuroRing supports modular single- and multi-FPGA deployment and is compatible with existing SNN workflows through integration with the NEST simulator. We evaluate NeuroRing on the cortical microcircuit benchmark and a Sudoku constraint-satisfaction workload. Results show that NeuroRing preserves the key activity statistics of the NEST reference model, achieves faster-than-real-time execution of the full-scale cortical microcircuit with a real-time factor (RTF) of 0.83, exhibits meaningful strong and weak scaling, and provides competitive energy efficiency on two programmable FPGAs. These results position NeuroRing as a flexible and scalable platform for both neuroscience simulation and broader event-driven applications.

</details>
