The methodology employs a three-party workflow involving Subject-Matter Experts (SMEs), developers, and LLM-based helper agents. These helper agents function as facilitation infrastructure, transforming informal domain intent into structured, reviewable specifications for human approval at defined gates. CARE addresses the "jagged technological frontier", characterized by uneven LLM performance, by bridging the gap between novice and expert analysts regarding domain constraints and verification practices. By generating concrete artifacts, including interaction requirements, reasoning policies, and evaluation criteria, CARE ensures agent behavior is specifiable, testable, and maintainable. Evaluation results from a scientific use case demonstrate that this stage-gated, artifact-driven methodology yields measurable improvements in development efficiency and complex-query performance.

</details>


### [207] [Agent-Agnostic Evaluation of SQL Accuracy in Production Text-to-SQL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28049)
*Taslim Jamal Arif,Kuldeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了STEF，一种生产环境原生的Text-to-SQL评估框架，仅依赖自然语言输入和生成的SQL进行评估，无需数据库模式或参考查询，首次实现了大规模的结构化查询评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-SQL评估方法（基于规则的SQL匹配或依赖模式的语义解析器）需要访问真实查询和结构化数据库模式，这些条件在生产环境中难以满足，导致生产环境的T2SQL代理缺乏有效评估，造成质量无声退化且无反馈机制。

Method: STEF框架从自然语言和SQL表示中提取语义规范，执行归一化特征对齐，通过包含过滤器对齐、语义判决和评估器置信度的复合指标生成0到100的可解释准确性分数。支持增强问题质量验证、可配置的应用特定规则注入以及生产稳健的归一化处理。

Result: STEF框架能够在无模式依赖的情况下实现持续的生产监控和代理改进反馈循环，经验证可扩展且有效，首次使大规模结构化查询评估成为可能。

Conclusion: STEF通过消除对数据库模式和参考查询的依赖，解决了生产环境中Text-to-SQL评估的根本挑战，为生产T2SQL系统提供了可行的持续评估和改进机制。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL (T2SQL) evaluation in production environments poses fundamental challenges that existing benchmarks do not address. Current evaluation methodologies whether rule-based SQL matching or schema-dependent semantic parsers assume access to ground-truth queries and structured database schema, constraints that are rarely satisfied in real-world deployments. This disconnect leaves production T2SQL agents largely unevaluated beyond developer-time testing, creating silent quality degradation with no feedback mechanism for continuous improvement. We present STEF (Schema-agnostic Text-to-SQL Evaluation Framework), a production-native evaluation system that operates exclusively on natural language inputs the user question, an enriched reformulation, and the generated SQL without requiring database schema or reference queries. STEF extracts semantic specifications from both natural language and SQL representations, performs normalized feature alignment, and produces an interpretable 0 to 100 accuracy score via a composite metric that encompasses filter alignment, semantic verdict, and confidence of the evaluator. Key contributions include: enriched question quality validation as a first-class evaluation signal, configurable application-specific rule injection via prompt templating, and production-robust normalization handling GROUP BY tolerance, ORDER BY defaults, and LIMIT heuristics. Empirical results demonstrate that STEF enables continuous production monitoring and agent improvement feedback loops without schema dependency, making structured query evaluation viable at scale for the first time.

</details>


### [208] [RHyVE: Competence-Aware Verification and Phase-Aware Deployment for LLM-Generated Reward Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28056)
*Feiyu Wu,Xu Zheng,Zhuocheng Wang,Yi ming Dai,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RHyVE协议，通过能力感知验证和阶段感知部署解决LLM生成奖励的可靠性问题，发现奖励排名在低能力下不可靠，且不存在通用的最优调度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLMs)使强化学习中的奖励设计更具可扩展性，但生成的奖励并非自动可靠的训练目标。现有工作主要关注奖励候选的生成、演化或选择，而较少关注这些候选何时可以在策略优化过程中被验证和部署。

Method: 提出RHyVE协议：(1) 将生成的奖励视为奖励假设，其效用取决于当前策略的能力和训练阶段；(2) 使用短视野分叉验证比较来自共享策略检查点的小型奖励假设集；(3) 实施能力感知验证和阶段感知部署。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 奖励排名在低能力时不可靠，但在任务相关阈值后变得有效；(2) 在稀疏操作任务中，阶段感知部署提高了锁定协议下的峰值和保留性能；(3) 生成的候选池表现出依赖阶段的获胜者变化，但没有固定的预热调度是普遍最优的；(4) RHyVE最好被理解为验证告知的部署协议而非通用调度器。

Conclusion: 奖励生成和奖励部署应作为耦合问题研究：生成的奖励必须在不断变化的策略能力下进行验证和部署。该方法在密集奖励和全失败边界实验中界定了适用范围。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) make reward design in reinforcement learning substantially more scalable, but generated rewards are not automatically reliable training objectives. Existing work has focused primarily on generating, evolving, or selecting reward candidates, while paying less attention to when such candidates can be verified and deployed during policy optimization. We study this deployment-time problem by treating generated rewards as reward hypotheses whose utility depends on the competence of the current policy and the phase of training. We propose \textsc{RHyVE}, a competence-aware verification and phase-aware deployment protocol that compares small sets of reward hypotheses from shared policy checkpoints using short-horizon fork verification. Our experiments show that reward rankings are unreliable at low competence but become informative after task-dependent thresholds. On a sparse manipulation task, phase-aware deployment improves peak and retained performance under a locked protocol. Updated LLM-generated reward-candidate experiments show candidate-family-dependent behavior: generated pools can exhibit phase-dependent winner changes, but no fixed warm-up schedule is universally optimal. Held-out schedule selection, conservative selector baselines, compute-matched controls, and scale controls further show that \textsc{RHyVE} is best understood as a verification-informed deployment protocol rather than a universal scheduler. Dense and all-failure boundary experiments delimit the scope of the method. Together, these results suggest that reward generation and reward deployment should be studied as coupled problems: generated rewards must be verified and deployed under changing policy competence.

</details>


### [209] [Characterizing the Consistency of the Emergent Misalignment Persona](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28082)
*Anietta Weckauff,Yuchen Zhang,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了涌现性错位现象中模型行为与自我认知的一致性，发现存在两种模式：一致人格模型（有害行为与自我报告错位相耦合）和倒置人格模型（产生有害输出但自认为是安全系统）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现涌现性错位模型中存在有害行为与自我评估的相关性，但目前尚不清楚这种对应关系在不同任务和微调领域中的稳定性如何。作者旨在刻画EM人格的一致性，以揭示微调数据对模型行为与自我认知关系的深层影响。

Method: 作者在六个狭窄错位领域（如不安全代码、风险金融建议、不良医疗建议）上对Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct进行微调，并通过五类实验进行评估：有害性评估、自我评估、AI系统描述选择、输出识别和分数预测。通过对比分析不同微调领域中模型行为与自我报告之间的关系模式。

Result: 研究发现了两种截然不同的模式：一致人格模型，其有害行为与自我报告的错位相互耦合；倒置人格模型，虽然产生有害输出，但自我识别为已对齐的AI系统。这表明EM人格在不同微调领域中表现出不同的行为-认知耦合特征。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个更细粒度的涌现性错位效应图景，质疑了EM人格的一致性假设。模型的有害行为与自我认知之间的关系并非简单的线性对应，而是呈现出更复杂的模式分化，这对理解和防范模型错位具有重要意义。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly misaligned data generalizes to broadly misaligned behavior, a phenomenon termed emergent misalignment (EM). While prior work has found a correlation between harmful behavior and self-assessment in emergently misaligned models, it remains unclear how consistent this correspondence is across tasks and whether it varies across fine-tuning domains. We characterize the consistency of the EM persona by fine-tuning Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned domains (e.g., insecure code, risky financial advice, bad medical advice) and administering experiments including harmfulness evaluation, self-assessment, choosing between two descriptions of AI systems, output recognition, and score prediction. Our results reveal two distinct patterns: coherent-persona models, in which harmful behavior and self-reported misalignment are coupled, and inverted-persona models, which produce harmful outputs while identifying as aligned AI systems. These findings reveal a more fine-grained picture of the effects of emergent misalignment, calling into question the consistency of the EM persona.

</details>


### [210] [What Makes a Good Terminal-Agent Benchmark Task: A Guideline for Adversarial, Difficult, and Legible Evaluation Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28093)
*Ivan Bercovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出终端代理基准测试任务编写存在普遍误区，主张任务应具备对抗性、难度和清晰性，而非像编写提示词那样帮助模型成功，并分析了常见失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着评估环境市场的增长，任务发布速度加快，但验证逻辑缺乏充分的对抗性审查。作者发现大多数人像编写提示词那样编写基准测试任务，这是根本性的方法论错误，导致任务质量低下。

Method: 基于一年多来对Terminal Bench的贡献和审查经验，作者对比分析了提示词编写与基准测试任务编写的本质差异，系统性地归类和阐述了常见的失败模式。

Result: 识别出六大常见失败模式：AI生成的指令、过度规范的说明、文牍式困难、假设隐藏知识的oracle解决方案、验证错误事物的测试，以及可被奖励黑客攻击的环境。实证证据表明，主流终端代理基准测试中超过15%的任务存在奖励黑客攻击漏洞。

Conclusion: 良好的基准测试任务应当是对抗性的、困难的且清晰的，真正的难度应体现在概念层面而非环境层面。本文为基准测试维护者、任务贡献者和研究人员提供了实用的指导参考。

Abstract: Terminal-agent benchmarks have become a primary signal for measuring the coding and system-administration capabilities of large language models. As the market for evaluation environments grows, so does the pressure to ship tasks quickly, often without thorough adversarial review of the verification logic. This paper is a guideline for writing good benchmark tasks, drawn from over a year of contributing to and reviewing tasks for Terminal Bench. Most people write benchmark tasks the way they write prompts. They shouldn't. A prompt is designed to help the agent succeed; a benchmark is designed to find out if it can. We argue that good tasks are adversarial, difficult, and legible, and that a large class of common failure modes -- AI-generated instructions, over-prescriptive specifications, clerical difficulty, oracle solutions that assume hidden knowledge, tests that validate the wrong things, and reward-hackable environments -- are predictable consequences of treating task authoring as prompt authoring. We catalog these failure modes, argue that real difficulty is conceptual rather than environmental, and discuss recent empirical evidence that over 15% of tasks in popular terminal-agent benchmarks are reward-hackable. We hope this serves as a useful reference for benchmark maintainers, task contributors, and researchers using benchmark scores as evidence.

</details>


### [211] [Mapping the Methodological Space of Classroom Interaction Research: Scale, Duration, and Modality in an Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28098)
*Dorottya Demszky,Edith Bouton,Alison Twiner,Sara Hennessy,Richard Correnti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三维框架（规模、持续时间、模态）来映射课堂互动研究的方法论空间，并探讨了AI如何拓展该空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂互动研究长期分裂为大规模观察和深度民族志两种取向，缺乏统一的方法论框架来理解不同研究方法的优劣与互补性。

Method: 构建了一个三维方法论框架（规模、持续时间、模态），通过对比两项对话教学研究（Howe et al., 2019; Snell & Lefstein, 2018）及访谈主要研究者，围绕可操作化、可见机制、实践转化三个问题进行阐释，并分析AI技术对该空间的拓展作用。

Result: 研究在框架中的位置决定了其揭示与遮蔽的内容；该框架能够有效指导研究设计与工具开发，AI技术正在扩展传统方法论空间的边界。

Conclusion: 该三维框架为课堂互动研究提供了系统的方法论导航，有助于研究者理解不同方法的局限性，并为AI时代的研究设计与工具开发提供指导。

Abstract: Research on classroom interaction has long been divided between large-scale observation and in-depth ethnographic work. We propose a framework mapping this methodological space along three dimensions--scale, duration, and modality--where a study's position shapes what it reveals and obscures. We illustrate it through contrasting studies of dialogic teaching--Howe et al. (2019) and Snell and Lefstein (2018)--and an interview with the lead researchers, organized around three questions: what can be operationalized, what mechanisms become visible, and what translates to practice. We then examine how AI is expanding this space and how the framework can guide research and tool design.

</details>


### [212] [Splitting Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks and Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28112)
*Matti Berthold,Lydia Blümel,Giovanni Buraglio,Anna Rapberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对包含支持关系的双极集合论证框架（BSAFs）的新型拆分技术，涵盖了集体攻击、集体支持及其组合拆分，并证明了其在常用论证语义下的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSAFs通过结合集体攻击和支持，扩展了SETAFs和BAFs的表达能力，并能自然地刻画非平坦的基于假设的论证。这种表达能力的提升带来了对多样化拆分技术的需求，以更有效地处理复杂论证框架。

Method: 提出了三类拆分模式：基于集体攻击的拆分（推广了SETAFs的拆分技术）、基于集体支持的拆分，以及同时基于集体攻击和支持的拆分。为这些拆分模式建立了相应的理论框架。

Result: 成功建立了适用于BSAFs的多种拆分模式，并从理论上证明了这些拆分技术在最常用的论证语义下的正确性。

Conclusion: 本研究为BSAFs提供了有效的拆分技术，不仅推广了现有的SETAFs拆分方法，还填补了支持关系拆分技术的空白，为处理更具表达力的论证框架奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: This work proposes novel splitting techniques for argumentation formalisms that incorporate supports between defeasible elements. We base our studies on bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs) which generalize argumentation frameworks with collective attacks (SETAFs), as well as bipolar argumentation frameworks (BAFs), by incorporating both collective attacks and supports. Notably, BSAFs establish a crucial link to structured argumentation as they naturally capture general (potentially non-flat) assumption-based argumentation. The increase in expressiveness calls for diverse forms of splitting. We consider splits over collective attacks (thereby generalizing the recently proposed splitting techniques for SETAFs), splits over collective supports, as well as splits over both collective attacks and supports. We establish suitable splitting schemata and prove their correctness for the most common argumentation semantics.

</details>


### [213] [Normativity and Productivism: Ableist Intelligence? A Degrowth Analysis of AI Sign Language Translation Tools for Deaf People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28125)
*Nina Seron-Abouelfadil,Poppy Fynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判性地分析了当前手语识别AI系统存在的问题，指出这些系统建立在有偏见的数据之上，缺乏聋人群体的参与，实际上是一种"能力主义智能"，通过技术合理化过程标准化了手语，反而异化和孤立了聋人群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的手语识别和解释AI系统虽然旨在提供无缝准确的翻译，但实际上建立在有偏见的数据基础上，且在开发过程中完全没有聋人群体的参与。这些系统被听力正常的人群广泛使用和接受，但他们对手势语言系统中固有的文化、语义和口语特征一无所知，这引发了对技术系统本质的深刻反思。

Method: 采用雅克·埃吕尔（Ellul）的技术系统理论和技术欺骗概念作为分析框架，探讨技术人员如何将语言标准化为技术可以捕捉的形式（数据、统计、数学语言），以及这种技术合理化过程如何为了追求利润而消灭了交流的条件。

Result: 研究发现，AI技术通过合理化过程产生了规范性效应，创造了一个标准化的、大众化的人类模型，要求人类适应工具和技术环境而非相反。这种技术重塑了人性的含义，将聋人置于生产力和效率的目标之下，表现出明显的反生产力特征——异化而非解放，隔离而非滋养人际关系。

Conclusion: 本文提出"AI即能力主义智能"的概念，指出此类系统强调并强化了手语的羞辱化和边缘化性质。技术本应服务于人类交流需求，但实际上却通过标准化和合理化过程，进一步边缘化了聋人群体，违背了技术应有的解放性目标。

Abstract: Sign languages, of any geographical or accentual variation, understandably face continuous scrutiny under the ever present popularity of verbal dictation and audism. Through this, many potential problems arise with the current lack of accessible communication for those who rely on such sign languages for essential conversation. Such AI systems regularly take the form of recognition and interpretation models, designed to provide seamless and accurate translation. In reality these systems are built from biased data and created without any input from deaf communities. Such models are widely used and accepted by their hearing counterparts who remain ignorant to the inherent culture, semantics and colloquial language present in gestural language systems.
  This phenomenon is best analysed under the scope of The Technological System and Technological bluff by Ellul. Indeed, what is at play here is the standardization of language by technicians into what can be captured by technique: data, statistics, a mathematical language. For that AI technique to exist, sign language must be rationalized, in a search for profit that annihilates the conditions for communication and fails to capture the human experience of the deaf person. By that process, it presents normative effects, creating a model of Man, standardized, massified, and who has to adapt to the tool and technical milieu instead of the other way around, which we assume should have been the goal of such a technology.
  Technique thus reshapes what it means to be human, to submit deaf people to the goals of productivity and efficiency. In doing so, it exhibits clear counter productivity, alienating instead of emancipating, isolating instead of nourishing human relationships. Therefore this paper argues for the idea of AI as Ableist Intelligence, as such systems seek to emphasise the humiliated and marginalised nature of sign.

</details>


### [214] [Intern-Atlas: A Methodological Evolution Graph as Research Infrastructure for AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28158)
*Yujun Wu,Dongxu Zhang,Xinchen Li,Jinhang Xu,Yiling Duan,Yumou Liu,Jiabao Pan,Xuanhe Zhou,Jingxuan Wei,Siyuan Li,Jintao Chen,Conghui He,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Intern-Atlas，一个从海量文献中自动构建方法演化图（MEG）的系统，旨在弥补现有文献基础设施缺乏显式方法演化表征的不足，为AI驱动的科研代理提供可查询的方法演进因果网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究基础设施以文档为中心，仅提供论文间的引用链接，缺乏对方法演化的显式表示（即方法如何、为何产生、适应及构建彼此关系）。随着AI驱动的科研代理成为科学知识的新消费者，这一缺陷变得尤为关键，因为这些代理无法可靠地从非结构化文本中重构方法演化拓扑。

Method: 构建Intern-Atlas方法演化图，从1,030,314篇AI论文中自动识别方法级实体，推断方法间的谱系关系，并捕获驱动连续创新间转移的瓶颈。该图包含9,410,201条语义类型化的边，每条边均基于逐字的源证据。为利用该结构，提出了自引导时序树搜索算法来构建演化链。

Result: 构建了包含9,410,201条边的方法演化图，并通过与专家策划的真实演化链对比，评估结果显示出高度一致性。此外，展示了Intern-Atlas在想法评估和自动想法生成等下游应用中的效用。

Conclusion: 方法演化图作为新兴自动科学发现的基础数据层，能够有效支持科研代理理解和追溯方法演进，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: Existing research infrastructure is fundamentally document-centric, providing citation links between papers but lacking explicit representations of methodological evolution. In particular, it does not capture the structured relationships that explain how and why research methods emerge, adapt, and build upon one another. With the rise of AI-driven research agents as a new class of consumers of scientific knowledge, this limitation becomes increasingly consequential, as such agents cannot reliably reconstruct method evolution topologies from unstructured text. We introduce Intern-Atlas, a methodological evolution graph that automatically identifies method-level entities, infers lineage relationships among methodologies, and captures the bottlenecks that drive transitions between successive innovations. Built from 1,030,314 papers spanning AI conferences, journals, and arXiv preprints, the resulting graph comprises 9,410,201 semantically typed edges, each grounded in verbatim source evidence, forming a queryable causal network of methodological development. To operationalize this structure, we further propose a self-guided temporal tree search algorithm for constructing evolution chains that trace the progression of methods over time. We evaluate the quality of the resulting graph against expert-curated ground-truth evolution chains and observe strong alignment. In addition, we demonstrate that Intern-Atlas enables downstream applications in idea evaluation and automated idea generation. We position methodological evolution graphs as a foundational data layer for the emerging automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [215] [LLM as Clinical Graph Structure Refiner: Enhancing Representation Learning in EEG Seizure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28178)
*Lincan Li,Zheng Chen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型(LLM)作为图边细化器的癫痫检测框架。针对脑电信号(EEG)噪声导致现有图构建方法产生冗余或无关边的问题，该框架首先验证了LLM能有效识别和移除冗余连接，随后开发了基于Transformer的边预测器与LLM细化器的两阶段方案，在TUSZ数据集上显著提升了癫痫检测精度和图结构的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电信号(EEG)固有的噪声特性使得鲁棒的表示学习极具挑战。现有的图构建方法(包括基于相关性或基于学习的方法)由于EEG数据的噪声本质，经常生成冗余或无关的边，这显著损害了图表示质量并限制了下游任务(癫痫检测)的性能。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段验证LLM边细化的有效性；第二阶段开发鲁棒解决方案：(1)使用基于Transformer的边预测器和多层感知机构建初始图，为潜在边分配概率分数并通过阈值确定边的存在；(2)LLM作为边集细化器，基于节点对的文本和统计特征验证剩余连接。

Result: 在TUSZ数据集上的大量实验表明，该LLM细化的图学习框架不仅提升了任务性能(癫痫检测精度显著提高)，还产生了更清晰、更具可解释性的图表示。验证了LLM能有效识别和移除冗余连接。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将大语言模型的推理和上下文理解能力引入脑电信号图学习领域，证明了LLM作为图边细化器的有效性。所提框架在提升癫痫检测性能的同时，增强了图结构的可解释性，为处理噪声数据提供了新思路。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) signals are vital for automated seizure detection, but their inherent noise makes robust representation learning challenging. Existing graph construction methods, whether correlation-based or learning-based, often generate redundant or irrelevant edges due to the noisy nature of EEG data. This significantly impairs the quality of graph representation and limits downstream task performance. Motivated by the remarkable reasoning and contextual understanding capabilities of large language models (LLMs), we explore the idea of using LLMs as graph edge refiners. Specifically, we propose a two-stage framework: we first verify that LLM-based edge refinement can effectively identify and remove redundant connections, leading to significant improvements in seizure detection accuracy and more meaningful graph structures. Building on this insight, we further develop a robust solution where the initial graph is constructed using a Transformer-based edge predictor and multilayer perceptron, assigning probability scores to potential edges and applying a threshold to determine their existence. The LLM then acts as an edge set refiner, making informed decisions based on both textual and statistical features of node pairs to validate the remaining connections. Extensive experiments on TUSZ dataset demonstrate that our LLM-refined graph learning framework not only enhances task performance but also yields cleaner and more interpretable graph representations.

</details>


### [216] [Synthetic Computers at Scale for Long-Horizon Productivity Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28181)
*Tao Ge,Baolin Peng,Hao Cheng,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Synthetic Computers at Scale”的可扩展方法，通过创建具有真实文件夹层次结构和丰富内容的合成计算机环境，并在其中运行长周期模拟来生成高质量的训练数据，从而显著提升智能体在长周期生产力任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中长周期的生产力工作高度依赖于用户特定的计算机环境（如目录结构、文档、表格等），而这些环境难以大规模获取和模拟。为了解决合成数据创建的可扩展性问题，使智能体能够在真实、复杂的环境中学习和执行长期任务，作者提出了构建大规模合成计算机环境的方法。

Method: 该方法首先构建包含真实文件夹层次结构和丰富内容（文档、表格、演示文稿等）的合成计算机环境。基于此，系统运行长周期模拟：一个智能体根据环境生成需耗时数周的生产力目标，另一个智能体扮演用户角色，在文件系统中导航、与模拟协作者协调并产出专业成果以完成目标。实验中创建了1,000个合成计算机，每次模拟平均运行超过8小时，跨越2,000多轮交互。

Result: 该方法成功生成了丰富的经验学习信号。实验结果表明，利用这些数据进行训练，智能体在领域内和领域外的生产力评估任务中均取得了显著的性能提升，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 鉴于用户角色数据规模庞大，该方法理论上可扩展至数百万甚至数十亿个合成用户世界。大规模合成计算机创建与模拟为智能体自我改进和智能体强化学习在长周期生产力场景中的应用提供了极具潜力的基础支撑。

Abstract: Realistic long-horizon productivity work is strongly conditioned on user-specific computer environments, where much of the work context is stored and organized through directory structures and content-rich artifacts. To scale synthetic data creation for such productivity scenarios, we introduce Synthetic Computers at Scale, a scalable methodology for creating such environments with realistic folder hierarchies and content-rich artifacts (e.g., documents, spreadsheets, and presentations). Conditioned on each synthetic computer, we run long-horizon simulations: one agent creates productivity objectives that are specific to the computer's user and require multiple professional deliverables and about a month of human work; another agent then acts as that user and keeps working across the computer -- for example, navigating the filesystem for grounding, coordinating with simulated collaborators, and producing professional artifacts -- until these objectives are completed.
  In preliminary experiments, we create 1,000 synthetic computers and run long-horizon simulations on them; each run requires over 8 hours of agent runtime and spans more than 2,000 turns on average. These simulations produce rich experiential learning signals, whose effectiveness is validated by significant improvements in agent performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain productivity evaluations. Given that personas are abundant at billion scale, this methodology can in principle scale to millions or even billions of synthetic user worlds with sufficient compute, enabling broader coverage of diverse professions, roles, contexts, environments, and productivity needs. We argue that scalable synthetic computer creation, together with at-scale simulations, is highly promising as a foundational substrate for agent self-improvement and agentic reinforcement learning in long-horizon productivity scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [MARS: Efficient, Adaptive Co-Scheduling for Heterogeneous Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26963)
*Yifei Wang,Hancheng Ye,Yechen Xu,Cong Guo,Chiyue Wei,Qinsi Wang,Dongting Li,Tingjun Chen,Hai "Helen" Li,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARS系统，针对自主智能体工作负载在GPU-CPU异构资源协调上的挑战，通过全局调度和缓存优化，实现了最高5.94倍的端到端延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）正从独立的文本生成器转变为自主智能体的执行核心。这种转变带来了两大变化：时间上从单轮推理变为多轮LLM-工具循环；空间上从聊天规模的纯GPU执行变为仓库级的GPU-CPU协同执行。因此，协调智能体执行的异构资源需求成为关键的系统挑战。

Method: 设计并实现了MARS系统，包含两个核心组件：1）通过统一信息流建立GPU推理和CPU工具执行的全局可见性，外部控制平面将准入与执行解耦以防止异构资源超额订阅；2）内部以智能体为中心的调度器，通过优先处理延迟敏感的延续任务并自适应保留KV缓存状态（仅在热启动带来延迟收益时），来最小化端到端关键路径。

Result: 评估结果表明，MARS在保持近乎最大系统吞吐量的同时，将端到端延迟降低了最多5.94倍。将MARS集成为OpenHands编码智能体框架的推理后端，使端到端任务完成时间加速了最多1.87倍。

Conclusion: MARS有效解决了自主智能体工作负载中异构资源协调的关键挑战，证明了其在实际应用场景中显著提升系统性能的能力，为LLM智能体系统的高效部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as the execution core of autonomous agents rather than as standalone text generators. Agentic workloads induce a temporal shift from single-turn inference to multi-turn LLM-tool loops, and a spatial shift from chat-scale, GPU-only execution to repository-scale, GPU-CPU co-located execution. Consequently, coordinating heterogeneous resource demands of agentic execution has emerged as a critical system challenge.
  We design and implement MARS, an efficient and adaptive co-scheduling system that globally coordinates heterogeneous agentic workloads under coupled GPU-CPU resource pressure. By establishing holistic visibility across GPU inference and CPU tool execution via a unified information stream, an external control plane in MARS decouples admission from execution to prevent heterogeneous resource oversubscription. An internal agent-centric scheduler further minimizes the end-to-end critical path by prioritizing latency-sensitive continuations and adaptively retaining KV cache state only when warm resumption yields a latency benefit. Our evaluations show that MARS reduces end-to-end latency by up to 5.94x while maintaining nearly maximal system throughput. We further integrate MARS as the serving backend for the OpenHands coding agent framework, demonstrating its real-world effectiveness by accelerating end-to-end task completion time by up to 1.87x.
  Our source code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [218] [Affinity Tailor: Dynamic Locality-Aware Scheduling at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27915)
*Jin Xin Ng,Ori Livneh,Richard O'Grady,Josh Don,Peng Ding,Samuel Grossman,Luis Otero,Chris Kennelly,David Lo,Carlos Villavieja*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了Affinity Tailor，一种用户空间引导的内核调度系统，通过将按需调整、拓扑紧凑的CPU集合作为亲和性提示而非硬分区，在保持CPU利用率的同时显著改善了空间局部性，在Google部署中实现了chiplet系统12%和非chiplet系统3%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大型多核系统运行多个共享CPU的工作负载，调度器（如Linux CFS）通过负载均衡使每个工作负载在多个核心上执行，这削弱了微架构层面的局部性：工作负载失去缓存、分支预测器和预取器的重用机会，并增加相互干扰，特别是在基于chiplet的系统中，跨核心执行也意味着跨LLC边界的扩展。而严格的CPU分区虽能保证局部性，但在工作负载未充分利用预留CPU时会导致容量闲置。

Method: 提出Affinity Tailor系统，其核心洞察是：内核可以将按需调整、拓扑紧凑的CPU集合作为亲和性提示而非硬分区，从而在共享CPU的工作负载间保持局部性。用户空间控制器在线估计每个工作负载的CPU需求，并分配一个与需求匹配的优选CPU集合，该集合尽可能与其他工作负载不相交，同时跨越尽可能少的LLC域。内核将此集合作为亲和性提示，引导线程向这些CPU执行，但在必要时仍允许在其他地方执行以保持利用率。

Result: 在Google部署后，Affinity Tailor相比Linux CFS实现了几何平均每CPU吞吐量增益：chiplet系统为12%，非chiplet系统为3%。此外，更快的执行减少了内存驻留，每GB吞吐量增益达3-7%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，未来调度器应将空间局部性视为一等目标，即使以牺牲工作保守性为代价。Affinity Tailor证明了通过用户空间引导和内核协同的方式，可以在保持高CPU利用率的同时有效改善空间局部性。

Abstract: Modern large multicore systems often run multiple workloads that share CPUs under schedulers such as Linux CFS. To keep CPUs busy, these schedulers load-balance runnable work, causing each workload to execute on many cores. This weakens locality at the microarchitectural level: workloads lose reuse in caches, branch predictors, and prefetchers, and interfere more with one another - especially on chiplet-based systems, where spreading execution across cores also spreads it across LLC boundaries. A natural alternative is strict CPU partitioning, but hard partitions leave capacity idle when workloads do not fully use their reserved CPUs.
  We present Affinity Tailor, a userspace-guided kernel scheduling system built on a key insight: the kernel can preserve locality for workloads that share CPUs by treating demand-sized, topologically compact CPU sets as affinity hints rather than hard partitions. A userspace controller estimates each workload's CPU demand online and assigns a preferred CPU set sized to that demand, chosen to be as disjoint as possible from other workloads while spanning as few LLC domains as possible. The kernel then uses this set as an affinity hint, steering threads toward those CPUs while still allowing execution elsewhere when needed to preserve utilization. Deployed at Google, Affinity Tailor delivers geometric-mean per-CPU throughput gains of 12% on chiplet-based systems and 3% on non-chiplet systems over Linux CFS. Furthermore, faster execution reduces memory residency, yielding per-GB throughput gains of 3-7%. Our findings suggest that future schedulers should treat spatial locality as a first-class objective, even at the expense of work-conservation.

</details>


### [219] [Crab: A Semantics-Aware Checkpoint/Restore Runtime for Agent Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28138)
*Tianyuan Wu,Chaokun Chang,Lunxi Cao,Wei Gao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Crab是一个透明的主机端运行时系统，利用eBPF技术识别智能体回合中与恢复相关的操作系统状态变化，避免不必要的检查点操作，在保证100%恢复正确性的同时将检查点流量减少高达87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体检查点与恢复方法存在两个极端：应用级恢复仅保留对话历史但丢失操作系统副作用；全量检查点虽然正确但在密集部署场景下开销过大。根本原因在于智能体-操作系统语义鸿沟：智能体框架无法感知工具调用的操作系统影响，而操作系统缺乏回合级上下文判断状态恢复的相关性。研究发现超过75%的智能体回合不产生恢复相关状态，大量检查点冗余。

Method: Crab采用透明的主机端运行时架构，无需修改智能体或检查点后端。核心组件包括：(1)基于eBPF的检查器，分类每个回合的操作系统可见效应以决定检查点粒度；(2)协调器，将检查点与回合边界对齐，并将检查点操作与LLM等待时间重叠；(3)主机范围调度引擎，在共置沙箱间调度检查点流量。

Result: 在Shell密集型和代码修复工作负载上，Crab将恢复正确性从仅对话方法的8%提升至100%，检查点流量减少高达87%，性能开销控制在无故障执行时间的1.9%以内。

Conclusion: 通过弥合智能体-操作系统语义鸿沟并利用回合间状态变化的稀疏性，Crab实现了高效透明的检查点与恢复机制，为自主智能体系统的容错、即时执行、强化学习 rollout 分支和安全回滚提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agents act through sandboxed containers and microVMs whose state spans filesystems, processes, and runtime artifacts. Checkpoint and restore (C/R) of this state is needed for fault tolerance, spot execution, RL rollout branching, and safe rollback-yet existing approaches fall into two extremes: application-level recovery preserves chat history but misses OS-side effects, while full per-turn checkpointing is correct but too expensive under dense co-location. The root cause is an agent-OS semantic gap: agent frameworks see tool calls but not their OS effects; the OS sees state changes but lacks turn-level context to judge recovery relevance. This gap hides massive sparsity: over 75% of agent turns produce no recovery-relevant state, so most checkpoints are unnecessary. Crab (Checkpoint-and-Restore for Agent SandBoxes) is a transparent host-side runtime that bridges this gap without modifying agents or C/R backends. An eBPF-based inspector classifies each turn's OS-visible effects to decide checkpoint granularity; a coordinator aligns checkpoints with turn boundaries and overlaps C/R with LLM wait time; and a host-scoped engine schedules checkpoint traffic across co-located sandboxes. On shell-intensive and code-repair workloads, Crab raises recovery correctness from 8% (chat-only) to 100%, cuts checkpoint traffic by up to 87%, and stays within 1.9% of fault-free execution time.

</details>
